2026. 10.27
先週（10/19～10/26）発表の主な経済指標
米生産活動は堅調。AI関連投資を背景にコンピューター・電子
製品や機械などがけん引。一方、データセンター建設に伴う需要
増加が、他産業の生産や建設活動を圧迫する兆候も
コア消費者物価は再び2％超え。特殊要因を除くサービス価格
も加速し、人件費転嫁の兆し。連合は2027年春闘で6％中心
を要求。中小企業の余力に課題も、物価上振れに注意
注目点② サービス物価の加速で重み増す2027年春闘戦線注目点①AI投資の恩恵拡大、副作用もじわり顕在化
総合PMIは57.6と高水準を維持。新規失業保険申
請件数も19.4万件と低水準で解雇の動きは引き続
き限定的。一方で、住宅着工件数や住宅建設業
者の景況感は悪化、高金利の影響が継続

耐久財製造業は、軍需やデータセンター（DC）増
設の旺盛な需要が、他の製品の生産を圧迫して
いると報告ベージュブック
（2026/10） テクノロジー企業がDC向け電気設備の買い占め
を試みたが、供給業者は他案件への供給を優先
し、これを拒否
機械製造業は、DC向けに鋼材在庫が大量に消
費されるなか、鉄鋼メーカーによる供給制限が他
の建設案件の遅延を招いていると指摘
テキサス州
見通し調査
（2026/9）
製造業では原材料コストが上昇。特に鋼材価格
が急上昇しており、その一因はDC需要とされるベージュブック
（2026/9） 一部の製造業がアルミニウム・銅・鋼材の供給不
足や調達期間の長期化を報告
一部の自動車メーカーはアルミニウムや一部部品
の供給不足で生産が幾分抑制されたと報告

ベージュブック
（2026/7）
米国：AI関連を中心に製造業生産は堅調も、資材・設備の需給ひっ迫が他産業に波及
 米国の製造業生産は、AI関連投資を背景にコンピューター・電子製品、一次金属、一般機械などが堅調
― 一方、非耐久財や家具など消費関連の生産は軟調に推移し、業種間のばらつきが拡大
 FRBは、データセンター建設に伴う鋼材・電気設備などの需要増加が、他の建設案件や生産活動に影響していると指摘
―AI投資の拡大が関連産業の生産を押し上げる一方、供給制約を通じて他産業の活動を圧迫する兆候も
― 当面はAI投資による成長押し上げ効果が期待される一方、供給制約や価格上昇を通じた負の影響にも注意が必要
4

日本：全国コアCPIは再び2％超え。基調的なサービス価格も伸び高まる
5
 9月の全国コアCPIは前年比＋2.1％（前月：同＋1.7％）と加速。2025年12月以来の2％超に
― 原油高・円安等のコスト上昇もあり、家事用消耗品や教養娯楽用品等を中心に伸び率が拡大
― 一般サービス価格も伸び加速。シルバーウィークの並びによる宿泊料や外食費の上昇などが主因
 特殊要因を除いた一般サービス価格もじわり上昇
― 変動の大きい宿泊費や私立高校無償化による押し下げを除いた基調的な一般サービス価格に上昇圧力。人件費増の
価格転嫁が進んでいる可能性を示唆

全国コアCPI CPI一般サービス価格の推移

### Page 7

日本：連合は2027年春闘で賃上げ要求を強化。高水準の賃上げ持続と物価への波及が焦点
6
 連合は、2027年春闘賃上げ要求の目安を「6％中心、5％以上」と発表
―5％以上の賃上げを最低目標として明確化。実質賃金1％上昇の定着をめざす方針
― 中小企業は大企業との格差是正に向け「6％以上」が目標。過去4年のインフレで実質賃金が低下した分の回復も要求
― また、新たに、2030年に2026年の賃金水準を15％程度上回る中期目標を設定。持続的な賃金上昇をめざす
 賃上げ余力は企業規模・業種で濃淡。中小企業等で高い「賃上げ5％の壁」
― なお、6％に近い賃上げが実現する場合、サービス価格への波及が強まり、基調的物価上昇率が2％を上回る可能性
連合・春闘基本構想の新旧比較 春闘賃上げ率

基本構想」 より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

### Page 8

欧州：2027年度予算審議が本格化。生活苦によるデモが拡大する中、財政赤字は高水準
7
 2027年度予算審議が本格化。財政赤字は総じて高止まり
― 一定の防衛費・エネルギー関連支出を柔軟化する特例措置の適用後も、複数の国でEU財政基準で定める赤字幅を超
過。スペインは予算案を巡って議会解散。フランス・ベルギーも10/15時点でEUに予算案を未提出
 生活改善を求める抗議活動が欧州各国で発生。支出圧力の高まりは財政再建の障壁に
― フランスの学生によるデモを発端に、生活費の高騰や公共サービスの劣化に対する抗議活動は欧州各国に拡大。一部
は暴動に発展し、警察官と衝突する事態に
9～10月に発生した主な抗議活動主要国の財政収支実績と見通し
（出所） 各種報道より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

欧州：金融市場は政治・財政を巡る混乱を嫌気。不透明感は2027年入り後も継続する見込み
8
 金融市場は政治・財政を巡る混乱を警戒。主要国の選挙を控える中、不透明感は2027年まで継続へ
― 抗議活動が広がるイタリア、フランス、ベルギーの一般政府債務残高（GDP比）は、EU加盟国の中でも上位（それぞれ2
位、3位、4位）。金利上昇の背景は政治・財政への懸念
― 影響は為替市場にも波及。9月中旬以降、ユーロはドルに対して弱含み
―2027年にかけてフランス大統領選やスペイン・イタリアの総選挙など、欧州主要国で重要な国政選挙が相次ぐ。極右・ポ

然考量燃油成本占整體營業成本逾 2 成，油價上漲仍對成本形成壓力，
預估稅後淨利 424.74 億元(+95.3%Y oY)，稅後 EPS 19.62 元。 
 受地緣政治及供應鏈重組影響，預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 13.24 元：展
望後市，根據 Clarksons Aug -26 預測，2026 年全球貨櫃供給年增
4.6%，需求年增 3.8%，供給增幅仍高於需求，惟供需缺口較 2025 年
逐步收斂。預估 2026 年營收 4,735.4 億元(+24.9%Y oY)，稅後淨利
991.6 億元(+44.6%Y oY)，稅後 EPS 45.8 元。展望 2027 年，隨新船
陸續交付，儘管塞港情況仍可能限縮有效 運力，惟考量整體供給成長
仍高於需求，預期市場運價將逐步回歸常態。整體而言，預估2027 年
營收 4,097.3 億元(-13.5%YoY)，稅後淨利 482.4 億元(-51.4%Y oY)，
稅後 EPS 22.28 元。 
 維持中立評等，目標價 265 元：評價方面，以 2Q27F BVPS 元計算，
目前 PB 0.8X ，介於長榮疫情前區間 0.5-1.5X 中下緣，儘符合高股息
題 材 ， 然 隨 歐 美 傳 統 淡 季 到 來 ， 維 持 中 立 評 等 ， 目 標 價265 元
(2Q27BVPS PB 0.9X)。

長榮 為 全 球 貨 櫃 運 力 第七大 航
商，市占率 5.9% 
 長榮成立於 1968 年 9 月，實收資本額 216.5 億元。截至 3Q26，全球超過 1.3
萬位員工及 293 個代理行，航線擴及 101 個國家，總船隊數共 242 艘(自有船
比例超過 6 成)，預計至 2030 年仍有 72 艘船待交付。根據 Alphaliner 統計，
截至目前長榮為全球貨櫃運力第七大航商，市佔率約 5.9%。長榮 1H26 營收
結構為貨運收入 93%，船艙租收入 1.2%，其他 5.8%。

歐 美 航 線 運 價 優 於 預 期 ， 預 估
3Q26 稅後 EPS 19.62 元 
 長榮 Sep-26 營收 497.11 億元(+1.48%MoM，+63.75%Y oY)，3Q26 營收達成
率 105.5%，優於預期，主要受惠歐美航線運價走升，且旺季附加費及燃油附
加費挹注營收 。獲利方面 ，受惠運價上漲帶動獲利率提升 ， 惟中東衝突於Jul-26
一度趨緩後再度升溫，且考量燃油成本占整體營業成本逾 2 成，油價上漲仍對
成本形成壓力，預估 3Q26 毛利率 38.91%。由於營收規模擴大，費用率有望
下降 ，預估營益率 35.71%，假設 匯率 持穩 ，預估稅後淨利 424.74 億元
(+164.9%QoQ，+95.3%Y oY)，稅後 EPS 19.62 元。

歐美航線需求放緩 ，預估 4Q26
稅後 EPS 14.89 元 
 根據上海航交所 10/9 公告，上海出口集裝箱運價指數 (SCFI) 3,664.11 點
(+0.05%WoW，+215.76%Y oY)，隨中國十一長假結束，歐線運價短期反彈，
分別為遠東-歐洲線+3.41%WoW，遠東-地中海線+0.2%WoW；美線持平，遠
東-美西線+0.08%WoW，遠東-美東-0.04%WoW。觀察 10/11 公告跨大西洋、
歐 洲 及 跨 太 平 洋 航 線 運 力 分 別 變 動 -1.14%WoW 、 -1.88%WoW 及
+0.48%WoW。整體而言，隨歐美航線逐步進入傳統淡季，航商陸續調整運力
配置，其中歐洲線運力投放減少，美線則大致持平，顯示航商持續透過運力調
節支撐市場運價。

展望 4Q26，需求面而言，歐線隨傳統旺季結束，終端需求動能轉弱，預期後
續運價將呈緩步回落走勢。美線方面，旺季需求已近尾聲，且部分船班裝載率
已有下滑，儘管 Sep-26 川習會後美國調降部分消費品關稅，包括玩具、小型
家電及節慶裝飾品等，惟目前尚未出現明顯大型備貨潮。供給面而言，隨水情
陸續改善 ， 巴拿馬運河管理局預計自2026/10/15 起將每日允許通行量由 32 艘
回升至 33 艘，惟仍低於原先 36 艘水準。考量大型貨櫃船通行具一定優先性，
目前對船期營運影響相對有限，惟通行量尚未完全恢復，仍可能使美東線有效
運力有所收斂。另，美伊衝突及紅海通行風險仍存， Gemini 聯盟持續恢復部
分紅海航線，近期亦傳出 Premier Alliance 有望於 4Q26 恢復部分紅海航線，
若後續其他航商陸續跟進，繞航所吸收之有效運力恐逐步釋放，對市場運價形
成壓力。

整體而言，儘管近洋線進入傳統旺季，惟長榮歐美航線營收占比較高，預期整
體運價及貨量將隨淡季到來而回落 ， 預估4Q26 營收 1,350.87 億元(-8%QoQ，
+57.6%Y oY)。獲利方面，儘管油價仍維持高位，惟隨貨量減少，相關變動成
本亦有望下降，預估毛利率 37.9%。另考量長榮往年於 4Q 提列年終獎金費
用，預期費用率將季增，預估營益率 29.4%。假設匯率持穩，預估稅後淨利
323.53 億元(-23.8%QoQ，+279.8%Y oY)，4Q26 稅後 EPS 14.94 元。

2026 年有效運力供給受限 ， 預估
稅後 EPS 45.75 元 
 展望後市，根據 Clarksons Aug-26 預測，2026 年全球貨櫃供給年增 4.6%，需
求年增 3.8%，供給增幅仍高於需求，惟供需缺口較 2025 年逐步收斂。短期而
言，受到缺工及天候因素影響，部分港口作業效率下降，近期塞港現象增加，
有助吸收部分有效運力。展望 2027 年，在尚未考慮紅海全面復航之前提下，
Clarksons 預估全球貨櫃供給+8.8%Y oY，需求+3.5%YoY，供需缺口將再度擴
大；若紅海航線進一步恢復，繞航所吸收之有效運力亦將逐步釋放，供給壓力
恐進一步增加。需求方面，則須持續關注停戰協議進展、美國關稅政策變化及
通膨壓力對終端需求之影響。

展望 2026 年，長榮持續優化航線布局，強化東南亞、中東、非洲及中南美洲
等區域配置 ， 並透過長約布局降低市場運價波動影響，整體而言 ，元富預估 2026
年營收 4,735.4 億元(+24.9%Y oY)。獲利方面，預估毛利率 30.8%，營益率
25.4%，稅後淨利 991.6 億元(+44.6%Y oY)，預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 45.8 元。
展望 2027 年，隨新船陸續交付，全球貨櫃船隊運力持續增加，儘管塞港情況
仍可能限縮有效運力，惟考量整體供給成長仍高於需求，預期市場運價將逐步
回歸常態。整體而言，預估 2027 年營收 4,097.3 億元(-13.5%Y oY)，稅後淨利
482.4 億元(-51.4%Y oY)，稅後 EPS 22.28 元。

大立光短期營運受智慧型手機新機拉貨動能趨緩影響 ， 加上新產
品良率及綠電採購成本壓力 ， 預期第四季營收及獲利表現仍相對
保守。展望 2027 年，手機主鏡頭持續朝高規格升級，有利公司
發揮精密光學設計與製造優勢；另一方面， FAU 預計於 2H27
進入量產，隨 CPO 技術逐步發展，有望成為中長期新增營收來
源。 考量大立光具備高精度光學製造、 微米級對位技術及自動化
設備開發能力， 技術門檻高且具備跨領域應用優勢， 正面看待公
司中長期發展。綜合以上，研究部預估大立光 2027 年營業收入
為 706.92 億元，Y+17.08%，毛利率 51.37%，Y+4.53ppts，
營業利益率 40.04%，Y+4.98ppts，稅後純益 268.24 億元，
Y+24.96%，EPS 為 205.08 元。研究部考量儘管 2026 年消費
性電子需求疲弱，且 CPO 產品短期營收貢獻仍有限，然而進度
優於先前預期，隨新產品逐步放量，2027 年營運有望重回成長
軌道，給予大立光 38 倍本益比評價，目標價訂為 7,800 元，投
資建議為買進。  
⚫ 公司基本資料 
⚫ Largan 大立光電(3008.TT)公司簡介 
大立光電(3008.TT)成立於 1987 年，並於 2002 年台灣證券交易所
掛牌上市。大立光作為全球光學塑膠鏡頭領導廠商，主要業務內容
為研發與製造精密光學塑膠鏡頭，產品主要應用於智慧型手機，其
餘也包含平板、筆記型電腦、車用及其他消費性電子產品，在全球
光學鏡頭市占率約佔 30%。其總部位於台中市南屯區精密機械園
區，共有十座廠區及兩座興建中廠區。前十大股東持股合計
38.06%。客戶為 APPLE、華為、三星、小米、OPPO、VIVO 等手
機品牌廠，主要競爭者則為玉晶光 (3406.TT) 及舜宇光學
(2382.HK)。3Q26 出貨量佔比：20MP 以上約 10~20%、10MP 約

⚫ 產業概況 
記憶體成本飆升壓抑出貨，智慧型手機市場進入量縮價增 
根據研調機構數據 ，2026 年全球智慧型手機市場受記憶體供應短缺
及成本大幅上漲影響 ， 出貨量預估將Y-16.7%至 10.5 億支 ， 較前一
季預估的 Y-13.9%再度下修，並創下歷年最大年度衰退幅度，其中
2H26 出貨量更預估 Y-27.2%。 由於記憶體成本年增超過300%，品
牌廠難以自行吸收成本，陸續透過漲價及調整產品組合因應，帶動
2026 年智慧型手機平均售價 Y+27.6%至 581 美元，全球市場產值
反而 Y+6.3%至 6,130 億美元，呈現「量縮價增」格局。產品結構
方面，低階市場受到衝擊最為明顯，尤其100 美元以下機種因原先
毛利空間有限，品牌廠陸續縮減產品線，2Q26 該價位出貨量已年
減近 60%；相較之下，高階機種因消費者價格敏感度較低，且成熟
市場具分期付款等機制支撐，需求相對具韌性，帶動整體市場加速
朝高階化發展。 
展望未來，記憶體供需緊張預期至少延續至 2028 年，全球市場短

⚫ 營運現況 
新機拉貨帶動營收季增，良率及綠電成本壓抑毛利表現 
大立光 3Q26 營業收入為 156.51 億元，Q+14.53%、Y-11.46%，
主要受惠新款 iPhone 進入備貨旺季，帶動高階鏡頭出貨增加。毛
利率為 42.21%，Q-7.21ppts、Y-4.99ppts，主要受到新機導入初期
良率較低，以及綠電採購成本增加影響。業外收益 13.57 億元，其
中包含匯兌收益 1.97 億元，支撐整體稅後獲利季增。綜合以上，大
立光 3Q26 營業收入為 156.51 億元，Y-11.46%、Q+14.53%，毛

⚫ 未來展望 
拉貨動能逐步趨緩，良率改善有望緩解毛利率壓力 
展望 4Q26，預期 10 月拉貨量與 9 月持平，11 月起則隨新機拉貨
高峰結束而逐步下滑 ， 儘管客戶1H27 新產品已自 10 月開始拉貨，
仍難以完全彌補既有訂單下修影響，加上目前客戶需求能見度偏
短，第四季營收動能仍具不確定性。毛利率方面，儘管新機生產良
率逐步改善 ， 然而綠電採購成本持續增加 ， 相關成本最快須至2028
年才能反映於報價，預期短期毛利率改善幅度仍將受到限制。綜合
以上 ， 研究部預估大立光4Q26 營業收入為 155.21 億元 ，Y-9.86%、
Q-0.83%，毛利率 46.65%，Y-1.32ppts、Q+4.44ppts，營業利益
率 35.29%，Y-1.46ppts、Q+5.04ppts，稅後純益 54.91 億元，
Y-18.28%，EPS 為 41.98 元。綜合以上，研究部預估大立光 2026
年營業收入為 603.80 億元 ，Y-1.26%， 毛利率46.84%，Y-3.59ppts，
營 業 利 益 率 35.06% ，Y-3.47ppts ， 稅 後 純 益 214.67 億元，
Y+0.90%，EPS 為 164.12 元。

手機主鏡頭規格持續升級，高階光學產品支撐營運動能 
展望 2027 年，智慧型手機主鏡頭將有規格升級，帶動高階鏡頭設
計提升，目前相關產品已完成技術準備並持續送樣，有望隨新機導
入逐步貢獻營收。潛望式鏡頭方面，下一波較大幅度規格升級預計
落在 2028 年，目前已進入試產階段，2027 年則以既有產品出貨為
主。獲利方面，因綠電採購成本持續增加，短期毛利率改善仍將取
決於新產品良率及產品組合變化。

FA 預計 2H27 量產，精密對位與整合能力開啟光通訊新成長動能 
光通方面，FA 產品已通過客戶認證，FAU 則持續進行送樣及整合
驗證 ，FAU 預計 2027 年年中完成產線建置 ，2H27 正式進入量產。
初期以單排 FA 及 Grating Couplin 技術為主，並朝 FAU 與 Carrier
整合出貨發展 ， 提高單一產品附加價值 ， 多排FA 及 Edge Coupling
則持續開發 ， 預計2028 年後逐步導入 。 公司憑藉微米級精密對位、
自製自動化設備及檢測能力 ， 可望在高技術門檻的FAU 市場建立競
爭優勢。預期 2027 年光通營收貢獻仍處於起步階段，隨後續產能
與良率提升，有望成為中長期成長動能。綜合以上，研究部預估大
立光 2027 年營業收入為 706.92 億元 ，Y+17.08%， 毛利率51.37%，
Y+4.53ppts，營業利益率 40.04%，Y+4.98ppts，稅後純益 268.24
億元，Y+24.96%，EPS 為 205.08 元。

⚫ 結論與建議 
大立光短期營運受智慧型手機新機拉貨動能趨緩影響，加上新產品
良率及綠電採購成本壓力，預期第四季營收及獲利表現仍相對保
守。展望 2027 年，手機主鏡頭持續朝高規格升級，有利公司發揮
精密光學設計與製造優勢 ； 另一方面 ，FAU 預計於 2H27 進入量產 ，
隨 CPO 技術逐步發展，有望成為中長期新增營收來源。考量大立
光具備高精度光學製造、微米級對位技術及自動化設備開發能力，
技術門檻高且具備跨領域應用優勢，正面看待公司中長期發展。綜
合以上，研究部預估大立光 2027 年營業收入為 706.92 億元，
Y+17.08%，毛利率 51.37%，Y+4.53ppts，營業利益率 40.04%，
Y+4.98ppts，稅後純益 268.24 億元，Y+24.96%，EPS 為 205.08
元。研究部考量儘管2026 年消費性電子需求疲弱 ，且CPO 產品短
期營收貢獻仍有限，然而進度優於先前預期，隨新產品逐步放量，
2027 年營運有望重回成長軌道，給予大立光 38 倍本益比評價，目
標價訂為 7,800 元，投資建議為買進。

### Page 1

ab 12 October 2026
Global Research
Genius Electronic Optical
Core resilience with CPO upside optionality
Raise PT to NT$1,250 on potential CPO contribution and intact core business
We reiterate Buy and raise our  price target to NT$1,250 from NT$700, rolling valuation
to 2028E and moving from P/B to a P/E-based SOTP that separately values the core lens
business and the emerging co-packaged optics (CPO) business. We believe Genius is
making progress sampling three precision components inside the fiber array unit (FAU)

targeting FAU integrators such as Coherent and FOCI.  We see potential for the company
to qualify the leading FAU module supplier into NVIDIA's CPO program. At the same
time, its core business is outperforming Largan, growing +1% YoY in Q326 vs. -11%
YoY for Largan as its share rises on periscope lens. Compared with the pressure on
Largan's margins from green electricity prices, we believe Genius should maintain its
margin, as its main production site is in Xiamen, whereas Largan's production in Taiwan

is affected by higher electricity prices.
Core intact: periscope share gains offset muted smartphone demand
September sales of NT$2.67bn (−2% MoM, +1% YoY) took Q326 to NT$7.82bn (+38%
QoQ, +1% YoY), in-line with VA consensus. Genius grew 10% YoY in 9M26 against 2%
at Largan, which we attribute to share gains in periscope lenses for premium iPhones,
offsetting legacy price cuts and lower VR lens shipments. We still keep 7% below VA on
4Q26E revenue (NT$7.34bn, −6% QoQ) reflecting muted iPhone sales and no content

in the iPhone 18 Pro's new variable-aperture lens.
CPO component strategy offers a different route to market from Largan
Unlike Largan's pursuit of complete FAU assembly, Genius is targeting high precision
optical components within the module, including: 1) high-precision prism to collimate
light from the PIC into the fibers, 2) V-groove/key slot to precisely hold the fibers in place,
and 3) MT ferrules (high density plastic connector bundling and aligning fibers). We

believe Genius' customer is one of the key module suppliers on NVIDIA's 2H27 program
targeting over 50% share. Our base case models a complete-FAU market of US$1.85bn
in 2028E and US$4.45bn in 2030E, with Genius base case for US$185m and US$668m
representing 10% and 15% revenue share of the complete-FAU revenue TAM.
Valuation: Maintain Buy with  PT lifted to NT$1,250
We fine-tune our 2026/2027 basic EPS estimates from NT$37.2/NT$41.5 to NT$36.7/NT

$44.0 and introduce 2028E EPS of NT$49.25. We move from P/B to P/E SOTP as the core
business now delivers steadier earnings and CPO warrants separate valuation. Our NT
$1,250 PT comprises NT$745 base business  (15x 2028E EPS of NT$49.25) and NT$505
for CPO (40x base case NT$12.72 EPS in 2028E). Catalysts include qualifications at
leading FAU makers and the board approved 7mn share private private placement with
potential strategic investor (albeit 6% dilution if issued). Our US$1.8bn CPO value

compares to US$7bn implied in our Largan NT$5,000 PT.

### Page 2

Genius Electronic Optical UBS Research
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Could Genius' CPO products contribute meaningful revenue in a few years?
Yes, from H227E into 2028E. We see Genius' CPO strategy as feasible and lower-risk than Largan's.
Largan's goal is to supply the complete FAU; Genius is entering at the component level, supplying V-
grooves, MT ferrules and prisms to FAU makers. This lets Genius sell into multiple FAU designs and

integrators, limiting its downside if the market converges on a single winning architecture.
Management believes multi-sourcing is inevitable once volumes scale and has seen early indications
of potentially substantial demand from US optical players, including Corning and Coherent. Genius
has a partnership with FOCI and is pursuing other FAU integrators including we believe Coherent;
management suggested further updates could come in the near term. Some parts already ship in

small volumes, but we do not expect meaningful revenue until H227 or 2028.
Q: Will Genius benefit from further lens technology upgrades in the coming years?
Yes. We expect premium lens participation and product mix to support core revenue growth of 8.0%,
9.7% and 9.1% in 2026-28E. We believe Genius is gaining share from Largan in Apple's periscope
lens supply chain after initially lagging. Apple (70–80% of Genius' sales) sell-through units grew 9%

YoY YTD through August according to Counterpoint, and Genius' 9M26 revenue grew 10% YoY on
a similar trajectory. Largan grew only 2% in 9M26, with Q326 down 11% YoY. For the iPhone 18
series, Genius participates in every lens spec except the variable-aperture upgrade, where it stepped
back because of pricing pressure from Chinese competitors. Management says Genius has the
technical capability and could enter if future specs or design changes improve the opportunity.

Genius offers a combination of an established earnings base from smartphone lens and potential
exposure to the passive components required for optical interconnects. We remain constructive on
the lens business and see CPO as a potentially material addition from 2028. The component strategy
provides several routes to customers, but the valuation should reflect uncertainty over qualified
content, allocation and margins. Our Buy rating and NT$1,250 PT is based  on NT$745 base business

(15x 2028E EPS of NT$49.25) plus NT$505 for the CPO earnings (40x base case NT$12.72 EPS in
2028E). The CPO option for Genius is US$1.8bn vs. US$7bn implied in our Largan NT$5,000 PT.
Genius share gains are showing in reported sales, with YTD sales +10% YoY vs. +2% YoY for Largan.
The company is also participating in every spec except variable aperture.  For CPO, Genius delivered
samples of its prism, V-groove, and MT ferrule to FOCI at the end of May, with initial feedback broadly

on track and no major issues reported to date. Our checks indicate the company is also positioning to
supply one of the leading FAU module suppliers for NVIDIA's CPO program from H227.
At NT$1,060, the shares trade at 24.1x our 2027E core EPS and 21.5x our 2028E core EPS, factoring
some of the CPO opportunity in the multiple rather than in estimates relative to the 10 year average
17x P/E. Applying a 15x valuation to the smartphone lens business + 40x to the CPO option from our

scenario of NT$9.30 EPS in 2028E yields upside from the last close at NT$1,060 to a NT$1,250 share
price.
Source: UBS estimates

### Page 4

Genius position in the CPO value chain
Genius is targeting the CPO market as a component supplier while Largan is advancing
from components into full-FAU assembly. Genius is sampling three components for CPO
with FAU makers, including: 1) high-precision prism to redirect light from the PIC into
the fibers, 2) V-groove/key slot to precisely hold the fibers in place, and 3) MT ferrules
(high density plastic connector bundling and aligning fibers). The companies overlap in

capability, with Largan also having PMLA and fiber arrays but not getting into some
components like MT ferrules and V-Grooves.
Genius's optical design, precision tooling, metrology and volume-manufacturing
experience offer it a good entry point for this market. Commercial differentiation will
depend on reproducible optical performance, dimensional tolerances, thermal stability
and customer yield.  It has announced collaboration with FOCI and  we believe has

potential to also work with one of NVIDIA's leading module suppliers for its H227
program.

Annual FAU TAM reaches US$1.85bn in 2028E and US$4.5bn in 2030E in

our base case
We update our FAU (fiber array unit) TAM factoring CPO/NPO penetration into
AI networking switches, NVIDIA multi-rack systems, scale-up on compute and switch
boards, and non-NVIDIA accelerators.  We assume one passive FAU assembly connecting
a fiber array to a photonic engine with one unit per optical engine.
We assume one FAU per optical engine and 32 per switch ASIC in our base case,
matching NVIDIA's 32x3.2T engines on its 102.4T Spectrum-X package. Broadcom's

102.4Tb/s Davisson uses 16 engines at 6.4Tb/s, so our bear case assumes an even mix of
these two designs (24 per ASIC). We note that higher switch bandwidth does not
automatically increase FAU count if engine bandwidth rises at the same rate. Our 2030E
assumption maintains the 32 FAUs per switch based on bandwidth rising to 204.8Tb/s

Our base case for the FAU market sums up 4 opportunities for CPO/NPO attach:
1. Scale-out and scale-across switches
We estimate annual switch ASIC shipments x CPO + NPO penetration x optical engines
per ASIC x FAUs per engine. In 2028E, 25% CPO/NPO penetration or 1.0m 102.4T
switch ASICs at 32 FAUs each produces 8.0m FAUs. In 2030E, 55% of 1.1mn

204.8T switch ASICs at 32 FAUs drives a market size of 19.4mn FAUs.

2. NVIDIA's multi-rack connections for Rubin Ultra and Feynman
NVIDIA at its 2026 GTC San Jose proposed a multi-rack option to network together 8
Oberon NVL72 racks for the Rubin Ultra generation and 8 Kyber NVL144 racks.

Feedback at the time showed  possible transceiver or NPO connections for Rubin Ultra
and CPO connections for Feynman.  Our 2028 base case uses 110,000 racks, 15% multi-
rack adoption and 75% CPO/NPO penetration, giving approximately 12,400 connected
racks.
For 2030E, our rack count falls to 35,000 racks as we assume Kyber would have 4x the
number of GPUs, equivalent to 140k Oberon equivalents. We assume the 35,000 racks
at 55% multi-rack adoption x 80% CPO/NPO attach gives 15,400 connected racks. We

assume about 216 and 576 inter-rack FAUs per rack, respectively, driving a market
opportunity of 2.7mn FAUs in 2028E and 8.9mn in 2030E.

NVIDIA GPUs and non-NVIDIA scale-up.  We add 10%/5% for FAUs lost at downstream
attach and test and for field spares, driving a market size of in our base case of 12.3mn
FAUs in 2028E and 35.6mn in 2030E. At US$150/US$125 per FAU, that is a US$1.85bn

market in 2028E and US$4.5bn in 2030E (bear/bull US$0.5bn/US$3.5bn in 2028E; US
$1.4bn/US$9.0bn in 2030E). The expansion to 2030E comes from wider optical
adoption and greater content in scale-up systems, despite an assumed FAU ASP decline
from US$150 to US$125.

Scenario analysis on Genius CPO opportunity

### Page 7

We assume in base case Genius captures 10% of the TAM in 2028E and 15% by 2030E.
The base case in 2028E is consistent with US$30 of prism, V-groove and ferrule content
in half of all FAUs or US$15 average content spread across the FAU market.  The
2030E bull case (25%) or US$37.50 would require near full content of some of the sub-
components or otherwise ability to integrate more of the solution value by that time.
Our bear case assumes only 1% share of the opportunity in 2028E and 5% of the

opportunity in 2030E, with EPS even by 2030E falling to NT$4 and yielding limited
valuation potential for the shares.

Reiterate Buy and raise PT to NT$1,250 valuing in CPO optionality

We adjust our valuation method from P/B to P/E SOTP to better reflect the company’s
core business and its opportunity within CPO industry while the core smartphone
business is demonstrating more stable earnings to use P/E vs. prior P/B. Our PT at NT
$1,250 is based on NT$745 base business  (15x 2028E EPS of NT$49.25) plus NT$505
for the CPO earnings (40x base case NT$12.72 EPS in 2028E) with catalysts from
upcoming qualifications into leading FAU makers and potential strategic private

placement with board approving 7mn share private placement (albeit 6% dilution if
issued). We note Genius CPO option valued at US$1.8bn vs. US$7bn implied in our
Largan valuation.
Our price target uses 2028 earnings for both businesses, reflecting the modeled start of
meaningful CPO contribution. We apply 15x core EPS of NT$49.25 and 40x CPO EPS of
NT$12.72 for a NT$1,250 price target. The 40x multiple assumes the optical business
can sustain strong growth after its initial ramp; qualification, concentration and margin

risk limit the confidence that can be placed on that earnings stream.
In the upside case, stronger premium lens participation and manufacturing execution lift
2028E core EPS to NT$54.32. Faster optical adoption expands the 2028E FAU market to
US$3.46bn; 15% effective revenue capture and a 31% pretax margin produce
approximately NT$36.88 CPO EPS. Applying 15x to core earnings and 42x to CPO
earnings gives approximately NT$2,365.
In the downside case, weaker lens demand and margin pressure lower core EPS to NT

$44.29. Slower optical adoption leaves a US$0.46bn FAU market, of which Genius
captures 1%, producing only NT$0.29 CPO EPS. Applying 12x and 38x gives
approximately NT$540. The downside is driven by both a weaker core business and a
much smaller optical contribution.

Genius Electronic Optical UBS Research
Upside/Downside Spectrum
Upside/Downside Spectrum
Genius (3406.TW) trading at 1,060 at closed Oct 8.
Source: UBS estimates
UPSIDE (NT$2,365): Lens upgrades accelerate and Genius gains share in higher-end
specs. 2027E/28E sales grow 11.8%/9.1% YoY (base 9.7%/9.1%), and GM expands to
35.6%/35.5% (base 34.4%/34.3%) on better yields and mix. 2028E core EPS of NT
$54.3 (+10% vs base) at 15x gives NT$815. Faster CPO adoption and stronger AI capex

lift the 2028E FAU TAM to US$3.5bn (19.8mn units at US$175); at 15% capture, CPO
sales reach US$519mn and CPO EPS NT$36.9, worth NT$1,550 at 42x.
BASE (NT$1,250):  Core NT$745 (15x NT$49.25) plus CPO NT$505 (40x NT$12.72), as
above.
DOWNSIDE (NT540): Smartphone demand weakens, legacy price erosion continues
and share gains stall. 2027E sales grow 7.4% YoY with GM of 33.4%, and 2028E core
EPS falls to NT$44.3 (−10% vs base), worth NT$530 at 12x. CPO adoption is slow

(10%/3% CPO/NPO in scale-out, no ASIC attach), leaving a US$0.46bn TAM; at 1%
capture, CPO is worth NT$11.

Company Description
Founded in 1989, Genius Electronic Optical (GSEO) is a leading global manufacturer of
camera lenses, providing full service optical solutions, including mobile lenses, automotive
lenses and AR/VR optics. Its production sites are in Taichung (one plant) and Xiamen (two

plants). The Taichung plant mainly manufactures VR lenses, while the Xiamen plants produce
compact lenses for consumer electronics. Optical lens products accounted for 73% of 2023
total revenue, and others include TOF lenses, flash lenses, VR lenses and LED lighting
products. Its major client is Apple and it supplies lenses for iPhones and iPads.
UBS EPS and Consensus EPS
The UBS EPS is an adjusted diluted EPS metric. It is calculated using the UBS analyst's

interpretation of earnings suitable for valuation purposes divided by the diluted number of
shares. This may differ to the way the consensus EPS metric has been calculated.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our Genius price target is based on PE-based sum-of-the-parts model. Genius' growth is
highly leveraged to the overall handset industry and could be affected by volatility in handset
demand, as well as whether handset OEMs will continue upgrading their camera design.

Genius' margins could be affected by increasing competition and the rise of new
technologies, ASP pressure from customers and fluctuating material and labour costs.
Downside risks include competitors catching up in technology, yield rate issues for new
products and uncertain iPhone demand.
Our price target is based on SOTP valuation on the CPO opportunity and traditional lens
business. We think Largan's downside risks include: 1) ongoing pricing pressure from

smartphone OEM clients and increasingly intense competition among lens suppliers; and 2) a
slower-thanexpected smartphone demand recovery in China, and 3) CPO commercial success
below the market's elevated expectations. We believe the key upside risk is a faster-than-
expected CPO qualification process at Largan. In addition, if the company demonstrates
exceptionally efficient and highly accurate automation capabilities, it could support greater
market share gains within the industry than we currently anticipate.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

研 究  報 告                
晶呈科技(4768.TT) 
產業：化學工業 
氣體原料量產降本效應與玻璃加工產品放量，推動明年獲利高成⻑ 
 投資評價與建議 
考量三廠C4F6⼀級合成放量顯著優化⽑利率重回30%，且AI驅動TGV及CPO
加工產品步入規模化量產，⻑期動能強勁，參考台股半導體特化與先進封裝概
念股本益比30-35倍，因短期受高額研發費用投入、新廠折舊與轉虧為盈初期
致使當前本益比失真，故採用TGV產線滿載Forward PER。 
 重點評論分析 
特氣出貨回升，TGV量產放量：推估4Q26隨特氣出貨回升及客⼾去庫存告⼀
段落，營收可望季增73.7%至6.6億元，全年營收估增50.9%至18.6億元，玻璃
加工受惠AI高階封裝與CPO需求，GCB及One Piece Glass FAU已進入規模量
產，預估2H26營收較上半年成⻑230%，惟三廠延至4Q26試量產，特氣原料
成本改善遞延，⽑利率下修至23%，加上研發費用增加，使稅後EPS 0.29元。  
⼀級合成與高⽑利產品帶動獲利躍升： 預估2027年營收年增35.1%至25.1億
元，受惠AI ASIC大廠特氣需求放量、TGV及矽光子共同封裝相關應用擴產6倍
產能，並拓展生醫檢測市場，預計3Q27末相關產線陸續量產，Stripper隨認證
客⼾增加至9家，⽑利率因三廠C4F6⼀級合成與純化工廠預計1Q27放量，年
產能250噸，可降低外購原料成本，加上Stripper產能利用率提升，以及TGV、
生醫檢測及Stripper等高⽑利產品營收占比提升至20%，帶動年增7.92ppts至
30.9% ， 營益率升至12.5% ， 稅前盈餘年增936%至2.91億元 ， 稅後EPS 4.30元 。  
 投資風險 
⼀級合成新廠量產進度與高⽑利產品TGV 、 特化投產或客⼾導入進度不如預期 。

### Page 2

2 
| 公司簡介  
公司所做的玻璃通孔技術(Through Glass Via ,TGV)主要應用於大積體電路玻璃載板(Glass Core Su bstrate, 
GCS)(非 Glass interposer)，是做為⼀個玻璃核心晶圓加工廠(TGV Glass Foundry)，並將加工好 的玻璃核心層
(Glass Core Board, GCB)，交付給下游 IC 載板廠或封測廠後，在玻璃核心的外側壓合 ABF 絕緣膜，並製作精細
的銅線路 ， 組合成完整的 GCS ． 亦透過玻璃加工技術開發⼀體成型式 CPO 光纖束導基座(One Piece Glass FAU)。 
GCB 是數個晶片叢共用的超大玻璃地基，支撐所有晶片和並保持封裝的平坦和穩定，解決穩定性(平坦度與結構支
撐)的瓶頸，位置在整個封裝體的最底層，玻璃材料解決熱膨脹、曲蹺度、以及對封裝尺寸的要求極限，目前公司
的製作玻璃微孔的貫穿深度與孔道寬度(孔徑)的比值已達到深寬比 100:1，全孔立體型態增圓度可達 99%、板厚
可達 1.85mm，單位面積產值相較 Glass Interposer 多 25-30 倍，在硬脆且易碎的玻璃材質上實現百倍深寬比，
是公司透過獨家氣態分解融蝕技術，在微觀垂直孔道上的極致控制力。 
One Piece Glass FAU 是⼀種應用於 CPO(共同封裝光學)與超高速光通訊系統中光電整合模組，超微細光纖束的
精準對位組裝，專為插裝與固定直徑僅 125 微米(µm)的超微細光纖束所設計(直徑極細，大約三根光纖才等於⼀
根頭髮的粗細)，在 AI 晶片架構從傳統電訊號傳輸轉向光訊號傳輸的過程中，該產品主要解決(1)微米級光纖對位
極難與光訊號損耗問題，公司雷射改質與氣態蝕刻技術(LADY)，在玻璃上打造出高⼀致性的微孔結構，提供高精
度的光纖對位，顯著提高光耦合效率(Optical Coupling Efficiency)，可減少多件式組裝產生的累積公差(2)高溫運
轉下的熱膨脹與光軸偏斜，利用玻璃材料極低熱膨脹係數的物理特性，確保光纖對位結構在⻑期高溫運算下仍保
持極佳的尺寸，(3)光軸穩定性以及光電整合元件的結構精密性，採用⼀體成型(One-Piece)的玻璃基座設計，結合
TGV 製程的高垂直度與高精度特性，配合客⼾在 AI 系統架構升級中對光電整合元件的規模化部署需求。

外銷與特氣出貨拉動累計營業收入倍增，受費用提升致⽑利率承壓：1H26 合併營收 8.21 億元，YoY+141%，主
要受惠特殊氣體外銷出口的大幅增加，氣體出貨支數達 13,022 支，其中 2Q26 營收 QoQ-13.9%至 3.8 億元，主
因在於部分外銷出口出貨量有所減少至 6,294 支，受韓國等記憶體客⼾要求調整出貨影響，但⽑利率
QoQ+11.1ppts 至 24.4%，因低⽑利產品(海外記憶體客⼾的特氣)相對 1Q26 出貨較少，1H26 ⽑利率 YoY-
20.45ppts 至 18.5%，除了產品組合關係，另因上游⼀級原料合成⾃給率尚未開出，使得原料成本負擔較沉重，
2Q26 費用率從 17.6%提升至 25.6%，主要來⾃研究發展費用大幅提升從 7.1%提升到 12.2%，目的在於為竹北五
廠、頭份三廠等新廠建置進行關鍵技術研發，1H26 稅前虧損 0.36 億元，稅後 EPS -0.99 元。 
特氣與 TGV 放量帶動 2H26 營收優於 1H26：3Q26 單季營收 3.8 億元，ＱoQ-0.6%，推估 3Q26 鋼瓶出貨量
6,000-6,200 支，主要來⾃ C4F6 內外銷出貨持續，⽑利率維持 24%，推估 4Q26 營收 QoQ+73.7%至 6.6 億元， 
4Q26 鋼瓶出貨量 7,500-7,800 支，2H26 鋼瓶出貨合計約 13,500-14,000 支，比 1H26 出貨量成⻑約 7.7%，隨
著全球半導體大廠的新產能與新廠陸續開出，帶動對氣體 C4F6 蝕刻氣體、Laser Gas 等的剛性需求持續增加，另

### Page 3

3 
外 2H26 隨客⼾去庫存化使需求回補，帶動氣體鋼瓶出貨量提升，並受惠於 AI 伺服器與高階封裝和矽光子共同封
裝(Co-Packaged Optics, CPO)，使玻璃加工市場快速成⻑，帶動相關特殊氣體的需求同步提升。 
玻璃加工技術在 TGV Glass Foundry 領域應用，已成功建置兩大可量產的核心產品線，配合年底 2 條產線(月產
能約 1 萬片)到位與量產放量，預估 2H26 營收相較 1H26 成⻑ 230%，主因為 GCB 與 One Piece Glass FAU 皆
已跨越工程驗證，正式進入單月接單與出貨片數達四位數以上的規模量產的放量階段，預估單片 150x150mm(5
萬孔/片)價值約$500-700 美元。 
特化產品 Stripper 目前設有⼀條年產能 288 頓產線，目前已被客⼾認證達 15 個品項，其中 7 個已達穩定交貨，
驗證通過的客⼾數已增加至９家，預估 2H26 營收相較 1H26 成⻑ 3%，加計其他產品，預估 2026 全年營收成⻑
率下修至 50.9%達 18.5 億元 ， 由於三廠(C4F6 ⼀級合成工廠)投產時程 ， 從先前 3Q26 延後至 4Q26 進行試量產 ，
使全年⽑利率從原先預估的 35%下修至 23%，加上研發費用擴張，下修稅前盈餘年成⻑ 41.3%至 0.28 億元，稅
後E P S  0 . 2 9元 。  
⼀級合成放量大幅優化特氣成本結構，TGV 導入 AI 大廠放量，帶動獲利大幅成⻑：邁入 2027 年，預估全年營收
成⻑ 35.1%至 25.1 億元，主要成⻑來⾃特殊氣體核心業務持續受惠 AI ASIC 大廠需求放量，另外玻璃加工應用於
AI 先進封裝與矽光子共同封裝產品預計於 3Q27 末累計建置達 12 條產線並正式量產，推動營收規模呈指數級成
⻑，在生醫檢測應用(竹北五廠)的最新進程上，已於生醫園區簽約購入竹北五廠房，將玻璃加工技術應用於生產生
醫用檢測儀器，在玻璃培養⽫微雕出高⼀致性的 3D 微孔陣列，模擬人體內真實的立體腫瘤微環境(3D 全立體型
態載具(Booster))，針對 EGFR 基因突變陽性的局部晚期非小細胞肺癌細胞進行精準控制併用標靶藥物泰格沙
(Tagrisso®)之細胞檢測實驗體外評估(In Vitro) ， 提升藥物對癌細胞的抑制與殺傷成效 ， 並藉由開發的生醫檢測儀
器，作為癌症精準用藥的關鍵數據，預計五廠將於 3Q27 末正式開始量產，此應用將使玻璃加工技術跨足高⽑利
生醫市場，現有廠房空間至少可規劃 6 條產線，後續將視客⼾需求進行滾動擴充。Stripper 隨 2026 年認證客⼾
增加朝向產能滿載，以及二廠無水氟化氫(AHF)量產線與三氯化硼(BCl3)試產線裝機完成，四條產線年產值上看 3-
4 億元的效益下，穩定貢獻營收。2027 全年⽑利率則受惠頭份三廠的 C4F6 ⼀級合成與純化工廠預計於 1Q27 正
式放量，設計年產能 250 噸，特氣⾃給率將大幅拉升，改變過去依賴外購二、三級純化原料的成本結構，使成本
大幅降低，加上特用化學品業務在產能利用率拉升，高⽑利產品如 TGV 玻璃載板、生醫檢測儀器、Stripper ⾃製
放量的佔比合計提升至 20%，使⽑利率顯著提升 7.92ppts 至 30.9%，費用率隨營業收入規模擴大而稀釋至 18.36%，
營益率提升 9.75ppt 至 12.5%，稅前盈餘年成⻑ 936%至 2.91 億元，稅後 EPS 4.30 元。

衛司特 26H1 獲利翻倍，EPS 達 9.7 元，逼近去年全年水準，主因
AI 伺服器帶動 PCB 高階電鍍製程由可溶性銅球轉向不溶性陽極鈦
網的剛性替代，帶動陽極鈦網營收年增約 50%，加上東南亞新廠
陸續投產使 BOO 業務穩健放量，以及半導體設備銷售與維護大幅
成長。展望後市，隨著 PCB 產業往高階多層板推進、層數與複雜
度提升，而面板廠 「鋁改銅」 趨勢確立，產業規格升級使含銅廢液
處理需求大增， PCB、面板及半導體業客戶積極擴產，加上切入
京東方供應鏈，跨廠複製動能使未來成長具想像空間，BOO 再生
金屬服務 、 陽極鈦網耗材及半導體電解設備與維護三大業務皆具高
成長性，轉投資城鋒科技也將成為新的成長動能。綜合以上，研究
部預估 2026/2027 年 EPS 21/31.28 元，以未來四季 EPS 計算，
給予本益比 20x，目標價 500 元，投資建議為買進。

⚫ 26H1 獲利翻倍 三大業務齊揚推升營運 
衛司特 26Q2 營收 5.91 億元，QoQ+14.7%、YoY+71.5%，
稅後淨利 1.24 億元，QoQ+15.4%、YoY+124.6%，EPS 5.19
元；26H1 營收 11.06 億元，YoY+65.8%，稅後淨利 2.31 億
元，YoY+98.5%，EPS 9.7 元。受惠 AI 伺服器發展引發的高
階電鍍製程材料替代 ，26H1 陽極鈦網營收 YoY 約 50%，PCB
客戶東南亞廠區陸續投產 、 加上高層數高密度蝕刻帶動含銅廢
液量增加，使 26H1 BOO 業務 YoY+30~40%，而 26H1 半導
體設備銷售與維護達 1.07 億元，已接近去年全年 1.12 億元。
高毛利的半導體設備比重提升，加上平均銅價 YoY 約 40%，
帶動 26Q2 毛利率年增 2.95 百分點至 37.07%。

⚫ 規格升級疊加擴廠潮及新廠滲透 多動能推升 2027 年成長 
衛司特 26Q3 營收 6.12 億元，QoQ+3.7%、YoY+74.5%。
RecoCell BOO 業務面向 PCB 及光電面板產業 ， 截至9 月底 ，
共有 67 個 PCB 案場及 11 個光電面板案場 ，PCB 產業方面，
隨著產業往高階多層板推進 ， 層數與複雜度提升使每單位面積
蝕刻出的含銅廢液濃度顯著增加 ， 疊加客戶積極擴增國內產能
與東南亞新廠陸續投產，帶動大量含銅廢水處理需求；此外，
傳統可溶性磷銅球會隨電鍍耗損縮小且易產生汙泥，在 AI 伺

溶性磷銅球，已成為不可逆的產業趨勢。光電面板產業方面，
隨著顯示規格升級，帶動面板廠在高品質與高世代產線推動
「鋁改銅」 ，使含銅廢液處理需求大增，今年公司正式打入中
國面板龍頭京東方供應鏈，京東方新產線全面導入銅製程，衛
司特於今年 6 月開始進駐並貢獻月營收 700~800 萬，目前公
司僅服務京東方一個廠區，預計今年底或明年切入第二個廠，
並規劃在未來 2~3 年爭取 7~8 個廠區合作 ， 後市具想像空間 。
BOO 再生金屬服務約 30%與銅價連動，全球 AI 算力與電力
基礎建設的發展使銅價長線具剛性支撐 ， 高檔震盪的銅價環境
有利於衛司特業務發展。綜合以上，研究部預估 2027 年
RecoCell BOO 業務成長 20%， 陽極鈦網可望維持高達約50%
之成長動能。

半導體產業方面，衛司特銷售 RecoCell 電解回收設備與維
護，2025 年半導體營收 1.12 億元，隨台灣半導體大廠積極擴
產 ， 預估2026 年半導體營收成長至 1.8 億元 ，2027 年進一步
躍升至 3 億元，2026~2027 年連續兩年皆維持 60%~70% 的
高年增率。

⚫ 轉投資布局深化循環經濟生態系 
轉投資方面，衛司特今年初以 1.93 億元取得城鋒科技 51%股
權，主要經營廢鉛蓄電池處理與回收，擁有 1000 噸的鉛酸電
池月處理許可，26H1 實際月處理量約 400~600 噸廢電池，
26H1 挹注衛司特營收 9300 萬元；為了提升效益，26H2 城鋒
暫停運轉以執行產能改善計畫，預計於年底將月產能提升至
1000 噸並實現轉虧為盈 ， 後續將進一步導入新設備 ， 待27Q1
設備建置完成，將申請擴充月處理量至 4000~5000 噸，盼
27H2 獲批後開始放量。此外，衛司特董事會於 9 月決議斥資
6 億元 ， 以每股60 元認購天瑛公司現金增資案 ， 取得20.83%
股權，天瑛公司為台灣最大規模的硫酸銅製造商，衛司特將結
合自身的銅廢水回收技術與天瑛的特化品精煉技術 ， 共同打造
高純度硫酸銅的封閉式全循環供應鏈。

綜合以上，研究部預估衛司特 2026 年營收 23.41 億元，
YoY+69.9%， 毛利率37.1%， 稅後淨利5 億元 ，YoY+91.1%，
除權日 - - 
殖利率(%) 2.27

EPS 21 元；2027 年營收 31.64 億元，YoY+35.1%，毛利率

⚫ 結論與建議 
衛司特 26H1 獲利翻倍，EPS 達 9.7 元，逼近去年全年水準，
主因 AI 伺服器帶動 PCB 高階電鍍製程由可溶性銅球轉向不溶
性陽極鈦網的剛性替代，帶動陽極鈦網營收年增約 50%，加
上東南亞新廠陸續投產使 BOO 業務穩健放量，以及半導體設
備銷售與維護大幅成長。展望後市，隨著 PCB 產業往高階多
層板推進 、 層數與複雜度提升 ， 而面板廠 「鋁改銅」 趨勢確立，
產業規格升級使含銅廢液處理需求大增 ，PCB、 面板及半導體
業客戶積極擴產，加上切入京東方供應鏈，跨廠複製動能使未
來成長具想像空間，BOO 再生金屬服務、陽極鈦網耗材及半
導體電解設備與維護三大業務皆具高成長性 ， 轉投資城鋒科技
也將成為新的成長動能。綜合以上，研究部預估 2026/2027
年 EPS 21/31.28 元 ， 以未來四季EPS 計算 ， 給予本益比20x，
目標價 500 元，投資建議為買進。

### Page 1

2026.09.21   
LG에너지솔루션 
(373220)

원-달러 환율 하락에도 실적 개선세 지속 Company Brief

3Q26 원/달러 환율 하락에도 불구하고 영업이익 시장 기대치 상회 전망 
동사의 3Q26 실적은 매출 8.8조원(+45% YoY, + 16% QoQ), 영업이익 3,690억원(-
39% YoY, +226% QoQ)을 기록하며 원/달러 환율 하락으로 매출은 시장 기대치를 
소폭 하회하겠으나 영업이익은 상회할 것으로 전망된다. AMPC 는 미국 ESS 생산량 
증가와 GM 얼티엄셀즈 재가동에 힘입어 전분기 대비 약 57% 증가한 3,790억원으로 
추정된다. 미국 상호관세 위법 판결에 따른 관세 환급금이나 완성차 업체들의 최소 
구매 물량 미달 보상금 반영 여부에 따라 추가적인 이익 개선 가능성도 존재한다. ESS 
부문은 전분기에서 이연된 물량의 출하가 본격화되면서 3Q26 매출이 전분기 대비 약 
38% 증가할 것으로 예상된다. 특히 미국 ESS 생산라인의 가동 안정화와 AMPC 수혜 
확대에 힘입어 수익성도 점진적으로 개선될 것으로 보인다. 자동차전지 부문도 
3Q26부터 회복세가 강화될 것으로 예상된다. 유럽향 물량 회복과 GM 얼티엄셀즈 
재가동으로 매출이 전분기 대비 큰 폭으로 증가하고, 고정비 부담 완화로 적자폭도 
뚜렷하게 축소될 전망이다. 소형전지 부문은 주요 고객사인 Tesla의 판매 호조에 따른 
안정적인 출하 증가로 매출 회복과 수익성 방어가 가능할 것으로 예상된다. 
4Q26 대규모 ESS 신규 수주 기대 
전세계 ESS 시장은 신재생 에너지 발전 확대와 AIDC 투자 본격화에 힘입어 빠르게 
성장하고 있다. 시장조사기관 SNE리서치에 따르면 1H26 전세계 ESS 배터리 
출하량은 461GWh로 전년 동기 대비 약 71% 증가했다. 이 중 북미 출하량은 
76GWh로 전년 동기 대비 약 83% 증가했으며, 분기별로는 1Q26 35GWh(+91% 
YoY), 2Q26 41GWh(+76% YoY) 를 기록했다. 통상 하반기에 출하가 집중되는 
계절성을 감안하면 올해 북미 ESS 배터리 출하량은 전년 대비 약 70% 증가한 
170~180GWh에 이를 것으로 전망된다. 연초 동사가 예상한 올해 북미 ESS 수요 
성장률 10% 초반 수준을 크게 상회할 가능성이 높은 상황이다. 올해 동사의 ESS 
신규 수주 목표는 약 90GWh이며, 현재까지 약 30GWh의 수주를 확정한 것으로 
파악된다. 이에 시장 일각에서는 연간 수주 목표 달성 여부에 대한 우려가 제기되고 
있다. 그러나 당사가 파악한 바에 따르면 대규모 ESS 신규 수주가 4Q26에 집중될 
것으로 예상된다. 특히 북미 ESS 수요의 가파른 증가세와 투자세액공제(48E)에 
연계된 PFE 공급망 규제 시행은 동사의 수주 확보에 우호적으로 작용할 전망이다. 
매수 투자의견과 목표주가 유지 
동사에 대한 매수 투자의견과 목표주가를 유지한다. 향후 국내 배터리 셀 업체들의 
북미 ESS 신규 수주가 지속적으로 확대될 전망이다. 미국 ESS 시장에서는 신재생 
에너지 발전 확대와 AIDC 투자 본격화에 따른 수요 증가, 중국 공급망에 대한 규제 
강화가 맞물리면서 국내 업체들에 우호적인 사업 환경이 조성되고 있다. 동사 역시 
ESS 중심의 생산라인 전환과 가동률 상승이 본격화될 경우 매출 성장과 함께 고정비 
부담 완화, AMPC 수혜 확대에 따른 수익성 개선이 기대된다. 이를 바탕으로 동사의 
영업이익은 2026년 약 0.8조원에서 2028년 약 5.1조원으로 가파르게 증가할 것으로 
전망된다. ESS의 역할이 신재생 에너지 연계용 저장장치를 넘어 AIDC의 안정적인 
전력 공급을 지원하는 핵심 인프라로 확대되고 있다는 점에도 주목할 필요가 있다. 
이에 따라 동사의 밸류에이션 역시 기존 EV 중심의 평가에서 벗어나 전력 인프라 
산업의 성장성을 반영하는 방향으로 재평가되어야 한다고 판단된다. 업종 내에서 실적 
개선 가시성과 정책 수혜가 뚜렷한 동사에 대한 비중 확대 전략이 유효하다.

Compliance notice 
당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

[투자의견] 
종목추천 투자등급  
종목투자의견은 향후 12개월간 추천일 종가대비 해당종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함.  
 Buy(매수): 추천일 종가대비 +15% 이상  
 Hold(보유): 추천일 종가대비 -15% ~ 15% 내외 등락 
 Sell(매도): 추천일 종가대비 -15% 이상 
산업추천 투자등급  
시가총액기준 산업별 시장비중대비 보유비중의 변화를 추천하는 것임 
 Overweight(비중확대)

TUẦN 21-25/09 [Điểm nhấn thị trường] 
VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tu ần qua, -30.55 điểm tương ứng -1.68%, với thanh 
khoản suy giảm

[Cập nhật công ty] 
FPT , REE, POW, AST , BWE

[Nhận định xu hướng] 
Áp lực điều chỉnh vẫn có thể còn tiếp diễn, tuy nhiên khi xu hướng tăng ngắn hạn 
chưa bị phá vỡ, VNINDEX đư ợc kỳ vọng sẽ nhận được lực đỡ tại vùng hỗ trợ gần 
quanh 1750, tương ứng vùng hợp lưu quanh SMA50, và có cơ hội hồi phục trở lại

### Page 2

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -30.55 điểm tương ứng -1.68%, với thanh khoản suy 
giảm. Dệt may, Tiện ích khí và Bao bì & đóng gói là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt 
nhất trong tuần. Khối ngoại đảo chiều bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị  
-2,865 tỷ đồng, tập trung ở các mã VHM, VPB và VIC; đồng thời, tự doanh đảo chiều bán 
ròng ở mức -146 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại bán ròng -3,351 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần qua nhưng 
đang duy trì vị thế mua lũy kế 1,650 hợp đồng. Tự doanh mua ròng 2,822 hợp đồng nhưng 
đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -6,636 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam bước vào tuần đầu tiên ở vị thế thị trường mới nổi bằng một nhịp điều 
chỉnh, khi VN-Index để mất mốc 1.800 điểm và lùi về đóng cửa quanh 1.785 điểm. Đây là 
phản ứng "mua kỳ vọng, bán thực tế" khá điển hình: ngay trong phiên nâng hạng chính 
thức 21/9, chỉ số mở cửa hưng phấn rồi nhanh chóng đảo chiều do áp lực chốt lời, với 
nhóm cổ phiếu Vingroup (VIC, VHM, VRE) giảm mạnh dẫn dắt đà đi xuống, và sức ép lan 
rộng sang bất động sản, ngân hàng, năng lượng về cuối tuần. Điểm đáng chú ý là dòng 
vốn ngoại đã tiếp tục bán ròng ngay sau cột mốc ngày 21/9, dù đã mua ròng trở lại trong 
tuần trước đó. Bối cảnh bên ngoài cũng kém thuận lợi khi Fed vừa tăng lãi suất và phát đi 
tín hiệu diều hâu, chứng khoán Mỹ điều chỉnh, giá dầu neo cao, và lợi suất trái phiếu 
Chính phủ Mỹ tăng vọt. Trong một, hai tuần tới, bên cạnh các yếu tố ngoại biên, mùa báo 
cáo KQKD quý 3 cũng sẽ bắt đầu tác động lên diễn biến thị trường chung.

Lợi nhuận sau thuế công ty mẹ tăng 
trưởng khả quan trong 2Q26 14% 
YoY, với động lực chính đến từ mảng 
dịch vụ CNTT nước ngoài

Mảng CNTT nước ngoài: Lĩnh vực dịch 
vụ IT phân cực mạnh mẽ do tác nhân 
AI (AI Agentic) s ẽ củng c ố xu hư ớng 
đầu tư vào AI

FPT ở vị thế thuận lợi cho phép 
doanh nghiệp đa dạng hóa công cụ 
AI và tối ưu chi phí vận hành

Khuyến ngh ị MUA với giá m ục tiêu 
86,000 VND/cổ phiếu 
 Do không còn h ạch toán FPT Telecom (FOX) là công ty con, d oanh thu 2Q2026 của 
FPT giảm mạnh còn 13,810 tỷ VND (-17% YoY). Lợi nhuận sau thuế công ty mẹ không 
bị ảnh hưởng bởi thay đổi hạch toán đạt 2,568 tỷ VND (14% YoY). Mảng dịch vụ CNTT 
tại thị trường nước ngoài tiếp tục là đ ộng lực tăng trư ởng chính, đạt doanh thu và 
LNTT lần lượt là 9,868/1,538 tỷ VND (16.3% /19.3% YoY).

Sự chuyển dịch chi tiêu từ dịch vụ IT truyền thống sang chuyển đổi số và tích hợp AI 
được kỳ vọng sẽ diễn ra ngày m ột mạnh mẽ khi: i) Sự phát triển của năng l ực tính 
toán giúp kéo giảm chi phí token (đơn v ị năng lực tính toán của AI) và thúc đ ẩy nhu 
cầu sử dụng AI của doanh nghiệp; ii) Các mô hình AI mã nguồn mở gia tăng lựa chọn 
cho người dùng sẽ thúc đẩy chi tiêu

Đối với các doanh nghiệp ứng dụng sâu các mô hình AI phục vụ hoạt động kinh doanh 
như FPT , chúng tôi nhận thấy xu hướng tối ưu chi phí tốt hơn nhờ khả năng sử dụng 
các mô hình AI đ ến từ đa dạng nhà cung c ấp; với chi phí trên hi ệu năng tính toán 
được cải thi ện. Trong k ịch b ản cơ s ở, chúng tôi d ự báo biên l ợi nhuận trước thu ế 
mảng CNTT nước ngoài sẽ ổn định ở mức 15.4% nhờ duy trì chi ến lược quản trị chi 
phí hiệu quả qua đó giúp LNTT 2026/2027 đạt 6,198/7,087 tỷ VND (13.3%/14.3% YoY).

Dựa trên định giá FCFF và P/E, triển vọng kinh doanh cũng như xem xét các rủi ro có 
thể phát sinh, chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA đối với cổ phiếu FPT . Giá mục tiêu là 86,000 
VND/cổ phiếu.

Các mảng kinh doanh duy trì ổn định 
giúp lợi nhuận sau thuế công ty mẹ 
tăng trưởng nhẹ

Sản lượng điện của REE tăng trưởng 
nhẹ nhờ mức tăng của mảng điện gió 
sau khi dự án Duyên Hải V1-4 vận 
hành

Mảng M&E ghi nhận giá trị ký mới cao 
trong 1H.2026, t ạo kỳ vọng hồi phục 
mạnh mẽ doanh thu

Mảng cho thuê văn phòng đứng trước 
cơ hội lớn đóng góp vào tăng trư ởng 
nhờ các điều kiện thuận lợi

Khuyến nghị MUA với giá mục tiêu 
58,100 VNĐ/cổ phiếu

Doanh thu Quý 2/2026 giảm nhẹ, đạt 2,432 tỷ VND (-3% YoY/-2% QoQ), trong khi l ợi 
nhuận sau thuế công ty mẹ đạt 632 tỷ VND (+1% YoY/-11.5% QoQ), do doanh thu của 
mảng bất động sản suy giảm. Qua đó, REE hoàn thành lần lượt 40%/47.8% kế hoạch 
doanh thu và LNST công ty mẹ 2026.

Trong Q2.2026, tổng sản lượng phát đi ện của REE tăng nh ẹ, đạt 2.8 tỷ kWh (+2.3% 
YoY) với đóng góp tăng trưởng rõ nét đến từ mảng điện gió khi dự án Duyên Hải V1-4 
bắt đầu vận hành; tuy nhiên  sản lượng thủy điện suy gi ảm do tác đ ộng tiêu c ực từ 
pha thời tiết El Nino, đạt 1.1 tỷ kWh.

Với năng lực chuyên môn được nâng cao, mảng M&E ghi nhận những dấu hiệu phục 
hồi tích cực trong giá trị hợp đồng ký mới trong 1H.2026, 5,865 tỷ VND (cao hơn 53.9% 
so với giá trị ký mới cả năm 2025). KBSV dự phóng doanh thu c ủa mảng M&E sẽ đạt 
4,499/5,190 tỷ VND (+28%/15% YoY) trong 2026/2027.

Hội tụ bởi 3 yếu tố: i) Định hướng phát triển kinh tế Hồ Chí Minh trở thành trung tâm 
tài chính quốc tế; ii) Hạ tầng giao thông ngày một được hoàn thiện và iii) Chi phí thuê 
cạnh tranh so với chất lượng dịch vụ của E. Town 6, KBSV dự phóng doanh thu mảng 
cho thuê văn phòng trong 2026/2027 tăng trưởng +14%/6.2% YoY.

Dựa trên đ ịnh giá SOTP , triển vọng kinh doanh cũng như xem xét các r ủi ro có th ể 
phát sinh, chúng tôi khuy ến nghị MUA đối với cổ phiếu REE. Giá m ục tiêu là 58,100 
VND/cổ phiếu, cao hơn 28.3% so với mức giá đóng cửa tại ngày 15/09/2026.

Doanh thu 2Q.26 bứt phá nhờ khoản 
thu nhập bất thường của nhiệt điện 
Cà Mau và Vũng Áng

Nhiệt đi ện than duy trì công su ất ở 
mức cao nh ờ nguồn cung nhiên li ệu 
ổn định trong 2H26

Điện khí Đông Nam Bộ (NT1&2 và 
NT3&4) có triển vọng hoạt động 
thuận lợi

Khuyến nghị MUA với giá mục tiêu 
18,100 VND/cổ phiếu 
 Trong Quý 2/2026, POW ghi nhận Doanh thu và LNST cổ đông công ty mẹ đạt lần lượt 
20,308 tỷ VND (116.1% YoY) và 3,519 tỷ VND (484.6% YoY) chủ yếu nhờ các khoản thu 
nhập từ chênh lệch tiền khí của nhiệt điện Cà Mau và tỷ giá của nhiệt điện Vũng Áng. 
Với kết qu ả trên, POW hoàn thành l ần lư ợt 65%/420% kế hoạch doanh thu và l ợi 
nhuận sau thuế công ty mẹ.

Chúng tôi cho r ằng nhà máy nhi ệt than Vũng Áng trong 2026 s ẽ được duy trì công 
suất huy động ở mức cao nhờ: i) Xác suất diễn ra El Nino tăng cao và dự kiến kéo dài 
đến đầu 2027, ii) Nguồn cung than trong nước được bảo đảm từ các đối tác (TKV, TCT 
Đông B ắc) và d ự trữ than d ồi dào c ủa doanh nghi ệp ghi nh ận ở mức cao trong 
Q2/2026.

Dự án đường dây 500kV Mạch 3 đi vào vận hành giúp giảm áp lực cạnh tranh của các 
nhà máy điện tại khu vực phía Nam, qua đó kỳ vọng doanh thu của NT1&2 trong năm 
2026 đạt 11,104 tỷ VND (5.3% YoY). Đối với dự án điện NT3&4, khoản thu nhập chênh 
lệch sản lượng theo giá công suất do sản lượng điện thực tế thấp hơn so với tỷ lệ Qc 
65% của nhà máy được duy trì sang 2H26 và 2027 sẽ giúp cho dự án ghi nhận doanh 
thu lần lượt 17,848/17,621 tỷ VND doanh thu trong 2026/2027.

Dựa trên định giá SOTP và EV/EBITDA, tri ển vọng kinh doanh cũng như xem xét các 
rủi ro có th ể phát sinh, chúng tôi khuy ến nghị MUA đối với cổ phiếu POW. Giá m ục 
tiêu là 18,100 VND/cổ phiếu.

CTCP Dịch vụ Hàng không Taseco (AST) 
Tiếp tục mở rộng điểm kinh doanh 
22/09/2026  
Chuyên viên cao cấp Nguyễn Ngọc Anh 
(+84) 24-7303-5333 anhntn@kbsec.com.vn

KQKD tăng trư ởng tích c ực trong 
2Q2026

Tăng trư ởng khách qua c ảng hàng 
không thúc đ ẩy doanh thu t ại các 
điểm kinh doanh hiện hữu

Mở rộng mạng lưới tạo động lực tăng 
trưởng doanh thu mới

Cơ c ấu kinh doanh và hi ệu qu ả vận 
hành hỗ trợ cải thiện biên lợi nhuận

Chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA, giá mục 
tiêu 76,500 VND/cổ phiếu

2Q2026, AST ghi nh ận doanh thu và LNST đ ạt 479/92 t ỷ VND (+22%/19% yoy), với 
động lực chủ yếu đến từ (1) đóng góp từ 19 điểm kinh doanh mở mới tại T3 Tân Sơn 
Nhất và (2) tăng trưởng tích cực của các mảng hàng miễn thuế, nhà hàng - fastfood 
và phòng ch ờ thương gia. Lũy k ế 6T2026, doanh thu và LNST đ ạt 1,035/221 t ỷ VND 
(+33%/46% yoy), lần lượt hoàn thành 48%/49% kế hoạch năm.

Chúng tôi dự phóng lượng khách quốc tế/nội địa tại các thị trường AST khai thác tăng 
bình quân 11%/5% mỗi năm đến 2030. Lưu lượng và tỷ trọng khách quốc tế gia tăng 
được kỳ vọng thúc đẩy số giao dịch và doanh thu bình quân trên mỗi khách, đặc biệt 
tại mảng miễn thuế, bán lẻ và phòng chờ.

Chúng tôi dự phóng số điểm kinh doanh AST tăng từ 145 điểm cuối năm 2026 lên 182 
điểm năm 2030. Sau đóng góp t ừ 19 điểm tại T3 Tân Sơn Nh ất và 6 điểm mới tại T2 
Nội Bài từ 2025 - 2026, các dự án Long Thành, Phú Qu ốc và Gia Bình ti ếp tục mở ra 
dư địa mở rộng mạng lưới trong trung và dài hạn cho doanh nghiệp.

Việc mở rộng các hoạt động mang lại lợi nhuận cao, bao gồm hàng miễn thuế, phòng 
chờ thương gia và tối ưu hoạt động tại các điểm kinh doanh đ ược kỳ vọng tác động 
tích c ực lên biên l ợi nhu ận của AST . Chúng tôi dự phóng biên l ợi nhu ận g ộp đ ạt 
64.7%/65.5% trong năm 2026/2027, hỗ trợ LNST công ty mẹ tăng lần lượt 48%/17% 
yoy.

Chúng tôi khuy ến nghị MUA đối với AST , giá mục tiêu 76 ,500 VND/cổ phiếu, tương 
đương tiềm năng tăng giá 20% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 21/09/2026.

Doanh thu 2Q2026 giảm nhẹ do 
không còn ghi nhận doanh thu 1 lần 
từ phí nước thải; trong khi LNST 
công ty mẹ tăng trưởng 1 chữ số

Tiêu thụ nước khu vực dân sinh đư ợc 
thúc đẩy nhờ thời tiết nắng nóng hơn; 
trong khi tri ển v ọng nhóm khách 
hàng công nghi ệp b ị ảnh hư ởng b ởi 
bất lợi thuế quan

Đơn giá thu gom, vận chuyện và xử 
lý rác thải được phê duyệt cho phép 
cải thiện doanh thu và lợi nhuận của 
mảng này

Khuyến nghị MUA với giá mục tiêu 
59,000 VNĐ/cổ phiếu

Doanh thu 2Q2026 đạt 1,222 tỷ (-8% YoY/18.1% QoQ) và LNST công ty mẹ đạt 325 tỷ 
VND (7% YoY/55% QoQ), ghi nhận tăng trưởng của tất cả các mảng. Mảng cung cấp 
nước tăng trưởng ổn định, tiếp tục là trụ cột chính của BWE, đóng góp doanh thu 785 
tỷ VND (+12% YoY/12% QoQ) nhờ nhà máy nước Long An v ận hành từ Q3/2025 giúp 
sản lượng nước tăng 28% YoY.

Cường độ và thời gian kéo dài c ủa các đ ợt nắng nóng trong 2026/2027 là cơ s ở để 
chúng tôi đi ều chỉnh tăng tăng trư ởng tiêu th ụ nước của nhóm khách hàng dân cư 
tại Bình Dương và Bình Phư ớc trong năm 2026 - 2027 lên mức 7.5/6.5% YoY. Trái lại, 
triển vọng tăng trưởng tiêu thụ nước nhóm sản xuất ảm đạm do tình hình thuế quan 
có diễn biến bất lợi trong ngắn hạn cho các ngành xu ất khẩu truyền thống của Việt 
Nam.

UBND TP .HCM công bố đơn giá thu gom, vận chuyển và xử lý rác thải mới, tăng mạnh 
từ 50-150% nhằm phản ánh đầy đủ và chính xác hơn các chi phí phát sinh trong thực 
tế. Chúng tôi cho rằng điều này sẽ tác động tích cực với BWE khi các dự án của doanh 
nghiệp ở mảng này s ẽ vận hành hi ệu quả hơn. Dự báo doanh thu và l ợi nhuận gộp 
của mảng xử lý rác thải lần lượt đạt 1,069/1,234 và 165.7/180.7 tỷ VND lần lượt trong 
2026/2027, tương ứng mức tăng 34%/15% và 27%/9% YoY.

Kết hợp 2 phương pháp định giá FCFF và EV/EBITDA, KBSV khuyến nghị MUA với BWE, 
giá mục tiêu 59,000 VND, tương đương với mức lợi suất kỳ vọng 42.2% so với giá đóng 
cửa ngày 22/09/2026.

Chỉ báo  Nhận định xu hướng Hành động

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX điều chỉnh trở lại trong tuần qua sau khi gặp thử thách tại vùng cản quanh 1850, 
hình thành cây nến tuần giảm điểm nhưng với phần bóng dưới khá dài cho thấy sự tham 
gia tích cực của lực cầu bắt đáy tại vùng hỗ trợ gần. Trên khung ngày, nhịp giảm mạnh giữa 
tuần đã khiến chỉ số đánh mất đáy ngắn hạn tuần trước và chớm cắt xuống dưới SMA200, 
trong khi phiên hồi phục cuối tuần chưa cho thấy sự cải thiện rõ rệt của lực cầu. Mặc dù 
diễn biến này cho thấy áp lực điều chỉnh vẫn có thể còn tiếp diễn, tuy nhiên khi xu hướng 
tăng ngắn hạn chưa bị phá vỡ, VNINDEX được kỳ vọng sẽ nhận được lực đỡ tại vùng hỗ trợ 
gần quanh 1750, tương ứng vùng hợp lưu quanh SMA50, và có cơ hội hồi phục trở lại. 
Kháng cự xa: 1880 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1830 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1750 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1710 (+-15)

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

Weekly Economics
U.S. Week Ahead
Existing Home Sales • Thursday
Housing market conditions remain highly unfavorable. Mortgage
rates are the primary headwind, and they have climbed even higher
alongside the rise in longer-term yields. At the same time, low
supply is preventing a broad correction in home prices, leaving
prospective buyers squeezed on two fronts.
We expect that existing home sales slid 3.4% in August to an
annualized rate of 3.92 million. Behind this forecast is ongoing

weakness in mortgage applications and a recent bounce in
mortgage rates, which rose from 6.43% at the beginning of July to
6.66% by month-end.
We do not expect affordability conditions to meaningfully improve
in the near term. Supply is likely to remain constrained while high
mortgage rates discourage mobility. And mortgage rates are apt
to remain elevated amid firm economic growth expectations and
heavy government and corporate debt issuance.

### Page 3

Economics Week Ahead Economics
CPI • Friday
Headline inflation looks to have picked up last month. We estimate
the CPI rose 0.40% in August, with gasoline prices increasing a little
over 4% as renewed tensions in the Middle East pushed oil higher.
Grocery prices also likely rebounded after modestly declining in July.
Outside of food and energy, price pressures appear little changed.
We expect the core CPI to rise 0.23%, essentially matching July's

pace. Core goods inflation should remain firm, led by another solid
increase in used vehicle prices as consumer prices continue to
catch up to the trend indicated at wholesale auctions. Meantime,
household furnishings and other tariff-sensitive categories appear
to be stabilizing after declining earlier this year.
Core services inflation should be little changed with another 0.2%
monthly increase. But underneath the surface, we expect a modest
pickup in travel (led by lodging away from home) to offset slightly

cooler medical care and housing cost growth.
All told, we expect the report to show that while headline inflation
remains influenced by the ebbs and flows of the conflict in the
Middle East, core inflation remains contained. Should our forecast
prove correct, core CPI will ease to 2.4% year-over-year, in line with
its 6-month annualized rate but above the more recent 3-month
annualized pace of 1.7%.

### Page 4

Weekly Economics
G10 Week Ahead
European Central Bank Monetary Policy Meeting •
Thursday
We expect the European Central Bank (ECB) to raise the Deposit
Rate by 25 bps to 2.50% next week. August headline inflation
remained elevated at 3.3% year-over-year, though softer core
and services inflation suggest underlying pressures continue to
moderate. Still, the more hawkish tone of the July minutes and
renewed rise in energy prices should keep policymakers cautious.

The recent escalation in the Middle East has pushed fuel prices
at the pump close to their 2022 peaks, while natural gas storage
remains below historical levels, leaving the Eurozone vulnerable
to further energy price increases. The ECB is unlikely to respond
aggressively if the shock remains confined to energy given the
downside risks to growth. However, a broader rise in food inflation
linked to El Niño would be more concerning, as highly visible prices

could lift inflation expectations and generate second-round effects.
For now, resilient Q3 activity, elevated headline inflation and
contained wage growth support one final hike. The ECB will likely
retain and emphasize its scenario-based outlook in the updated
staff projections given continued uncertainty around the conflict
and energy prices. The policy path beyond September will depend
on whether energy and food pressures persist and begin to spread,

which could leave room for further tightening.

U.K. Monthly GDP • Friday
We expect UK GDP to rise 0.2% month-over-month in July, with
growth of 0.3% on a three-month-over-three-month basis.

Activity likely moderated after June’s strong rebound, though
services remained supportive as the July services PMI rose to 52.1.
Retail sales volumes fell 0.5% month-over-month, while softer
manufacturing activity likely reflected some reversal of earlier
precautionary stockbuilding.
The moderation appears more consistent with payback from
June than a broader deterioration in momentum. August PMIs
remain consistent with Q3 GDP growth of around 0.3% quarter-

over-quarter. Still, the renewed escalation in the Middle East and
associated rise in gas and energy prices adds upside risk to inflation
and could weigh on household demand. Against this backdrop and
with Bank of England hawks continuing to emphasize the need for
tighter policy, we expect the BoE to remain cautious and continue
to look for a rate hike in Q4. -1.0%

### Page 5

Economics Week Ahead Economics
EM Week Ahead
Mexico CPI • Wednesday
We expect Mexico’s August inflation rate to edge higher, with
headline CPI rising to 3.34% year-over-year (0.27% month-over-
month) from 3.26% year-over-year in early August. Part of the
pickup likely reflects less favorable base effects and a smaller
disinflationary contribution from food prices. While agricultural
prices continued to help restrain inflation in mid-August, the drag

was notably less pronounced than in July. Core inflation should
remain elevated at 3.98% year-over-year (0.25% month-over-
month).
While inflation remains within Banxico’s midpoint 3% target,
persistent core and services inflation suggest convergence toward
the midpoint of the target could prove gradual. For now, we
continue to expect Banxico to keep rates on hold, with the policy
rate remaining at 6.50% through the rest of 2026 and into early
2027 as policymakers await clearer evidence that underlying

inflation pressures are easing sustainably. While rate cuts remain
a possibility in 2027, the current inflation backdrop argues for
patience in the near term.

Brazil CPI • Friday
We expect Brazil’s August inflation rate to slow to 4.20% year-over-
year, helped by lower electricity, food and fuel prices. The main
driver should be a temporary reduction in residential electricity bills
stemming from a one-off credit, while recent data already point to
a notable easing in price pressures. Mid-August CPI came in weaker
than expected, with inflation falling to 4.24% year-over-year amid
declines in electricity, food, fuel and airfare prices. Beyond these

temporary factors, restrictive monetary policy continues to weigh
on economic activity, as evidenced by the recent slowdown in Q2
GDP growth. However, underlying inflation risks remain. A still-
tight labor market and government measures aimed at supporting
consumption could keep services inflation sticky, making progress
toward the 3.0% inflation target more gradual.
Overall, the inflation outlook remains consistent with additional
policy easing. We expect COPOM to deliver a 25 bps rate cut in

September, bringing the Selic Rate to 13.75%. Beyond September,
policymakers are likely to proceed cautiously as they assess
incoming inflation data and the impact of the election cycle on
inflation expectations. Our base case remains for a pause after
September, with additional easing more likely in 2027.

### Page 1

Jio Platforms 上市⾸⽇股价飙升逾 21% ，最新报 1362 卢⽐，远超发⾏价 1119 卢⽐，成为印
度史上最⼤ IPO 。此次募资 3020 亿卢⽐，认购超 22 倍，⻉莱德、 GIC 等顶级机构参与。野
村给予买⼊评级、⽬标价 1340 卢⽐，但实际股价已突破该⽬标，显⽰市场对其⻓期盈利预
期更为乐观。
摘要：
Jio Platforms 上市⾸⽇股价飙升逾 21% ，成为印度史上最⼤ IPO ，全球顶级机构投资者的热烈追
捧印证了市场对这家科技巨头的⾼度信⼼。
股价最新报 1362 卢⽐，较发⾏价 1119 卢⽐⼤幅溢价。这⼀表现超越了机构分析师的普遍预期
—— 野村证券周三给予 Jio Platforms 买⼊评级，⽬标价 1340 卢⽐，较发⾏价隐含 19.7% 的上⾏
空间，但实际股价已⼀举突破该⽬标位。
此次 IPO 共募资 3020 亿卢⽐（约 32 亿美元），认购倍数超过 22 倍，包括⻉莱德、 Capital Group
及新加坡政府投资公司（ GIC ）在内的全球顶级基⾦均参与了锚定投资者配售。这⼀⾥程碑式的

上市，为印度资本市场的⼤型交易注⼊了强⼼剂。
史上最⼤ IPO ，开市 95 分钟售罄
Jio Platforms 此次共发售 2.7 亿股，发⾏价定为每股 1119 卢⽐，融资规模创下印度 IPO 历史纪
录。
据孟买证券交易所（ BSE ）数据，公开认购阶段整体超额认购逾 22 倍，其中合格机构投资者
（ QIB ）部分认购倍数⾼达 70.55 倍，零售投资者部分则认购 2.72 倍，机构需求远超散⼾。
Jio Platforms 董事总经理、印度亿万富翁穆克什 · 安巴尼（ Mukesh Ambani ）之⼦ Akash Ambani
在上市仪式前表⽰， IPO 于 10 ⽉ 21 ⽇开放认购后，仅 95 分钟便完成全额认购。含锚定投资者在
内，此次发⾏合计吸引了 780 亿美元的总需求。
⺟公司领衔， Meta 与⾕歌居前列
据 LSEG 数据，安巴尼旗舰企业信实⼯业（ Reliance Industries ）持有 Jio Platforms 逾 66% 的股
权，为第⼀⼤股东。 Meta Platforms 旗下关联公司 Jaadhu Holdings 持股近 10% ，位居第⼆；⾕

歌国际（ Google International ）持股 7.7% ，列第三。
野村证券周三发布报告，⾸次覆盖 Jio Platforms 并给予买⼊评级，⽬标价 1340 卢⽐。野村在报
告中指出， Jio 的资本开⽀周期基本已接近尾声，预计公司⾃由现⾦流将出现显著改善。

Document 359: Quantum dictionary, part 1_ Decoding the fundamentals From superposition to decoherence, we unpack the concepts and technologies shaping the future of quantum computing. 2 min read

Source PDF: all report pdf/Quantum dictionary, part 1_ Decoding the fundamentals From superposition to decoherence, we unpack the concepts and technologies shaping the future of quantum computing. 2 min read.pdf

Transformation 
Quantum dictionary, part 1: Decoding the fundamentals 
21 October 2026

Key takeaways 
• What is quantum computing? How does it work? What is a qubit? And when will quantum reach its full potential? Quantum 
computing challenges our intuition and our understanding of what computers can do. In this two-part dictionary series, we 
decode key terms behind what could be the next major technological revolution. 
• Qubits are the basic building blocks of quantum computers, harnessing properties such as superposition and entanglement to 
process information. However, qubits are highly sensitive to their surroundings where disturbances can lead to errors in 
quantum computation, making error correction a key challenge for building quantum computers. 
• There is no single approach to building a quantum computer. Researchers and companies are pursuing a range of qubit 
technologies, from neutral atoms and photons to spin, superconducting, topological and trapped-ion qubits, while quantum 
computing itself is likely to increasingly work alongside classical computing and AI.

First, let’s lay the groundwork… 
Quantum computing  
Quantum computing uses qubits and quantum properties such as superposition (defined later on) to perform complex 
calculations and solve certain problems faster than classical computers.1 From a practical perspective, quantum computing could 
be used to tackle problems related to optimization, machine learning, simulation and factorization across a range of sectors, 
including autos and logistics, financial services, energy and climate, health and life sciences and national security. 
Current quantum computers can perform ‘just’ thousands of quantum operations. As the technology advances from thousands to 
millions, billions and eventually trillions of operations, quantum computers could begin to have commercial applications.  
• Millions: Financial services and security could experience some of the first use cases, such as threat detection, fraud 
prevention, derivatives pricing, bond trading and arbitrage. 
• Billions: Quantum could help power AI through advanced data generation capabilities.  
• Trillions: Quantum chemistry applications could emerge, including drug discovery, battery simulation and materials 
discovery. 
These are the publicly known applications, according to Oxford Quantum Circuits (OQC). And as quantum computing capabilities 
improve, some of these use cases could emerge sooner than anticipated.  
Now, we’ll tackle the core concepts… 
Qubits 
Qubits (quantum bits) are a quantum computer’s basic unit of information. They can exhibit quantum properties such as 
superposition and entanglement (which we elaborate on below) — properties fundamental to quantum computing. 
Quantum gates manipulate the state of qubits, much like logic gates manipulate bits in classical computers. Despite the name, a 
quantum gate is not a physical object, but rather an operation applied to a quantum system that changes its state. 
From physical to logical qubits 
Physical qubits are the foundation of a quantum computer’s hardware. However, they are highly susceptible to error. To address 
this, multiple physical qubits can be structured together to encode a logical qubit, allowing errors to be detected and corrected

1 Schneider, J., & Smalley, I. (2025, June 10). What is quantum computing? IBM.

and making the stored quantum information more reliable. In recent years, according to BofA Global Research, logical qubits 
have become a greater focus for quantum computing companies. 
Entanglement 
Entanglement is a condition where two or more qubits become linked so that their states are correlated, regardless of distance 
(Exhibit 1). It allows quantum computers to process information in ways that can be difficult or impossible for classical 
computers. 
So, how does it work? Suppose we have two photons that have opposite polarizations (the orientation of their light waves). 
Before measurement, neither has a definite polarization. When one is measured as horizontally polarized, the other will be 
vertically polarized when measured in the same basis, wherever it is. When measured separately, they appear to be random, but 
when compared, they are correlated (Exhibit 2). 
The challenges 
Once created, entanglement must be protected from decoherence (where interactions with the environment destroy quantum 
correlations). Maintaining entanglement over long distances is another challenge. Photons traveling through optical fiber can be 
absorbed or scattered, making long-distance quantum communication difficult. 2 Quantum repeaters aim to overcome this 
challenge by extending entanglement across a series of shorter links. 
Exhibit 1: Entanglement is a state where at least two qubits become 
correlated

Superposition 
Unlike a classical bit, which can be one of two states (0 or 1), superposition allows a quantum system to exist in multiple states 
or configurations at the same time (Exhibit 3). For example, a qubit can exist in the 0 state, the 1 state or any state that is a 
combination of 0 and 1.3  
A quantum system can maintain a superposition until it is observed or measured. Once measured, its superposition collapses into 
a single state. Another way of thinking about superposition is to imagine a sphere with the north pole representing zero and the 
south pole representing one. Other points on the surface of the sphere represent different superposition states. This allows us 
to: 1) represent complex forms of information; and 2) simulate atoms in a computer. 
Schrödinger’s cat 
In 1935, physicist Erwin Schrödinger proposed a thought experiment to illustrate the strange implications of quantum 
mechanics. In this scenario, a cat, a radioactive substance that decays very slowly, and a Geiger counter are put together in a 
sealed box. According to quantum mechanics, the system can be described as a superposition in which the cat is both alive and 
dead until the box is opened. Once the box is opened and the system is observed, the superposition collapses into a single 
outcome.

Enabling parallel computation versus classical computing 
Superposition is a key attribute of quantum computing that allows for quantum parallelism, or the ability to perform multiple 
computations at the same time. Classical bits can only be in one of two states, 0 or 1. In contrast, a qubit can be in a 
superposition of 0 and 1, allowing a quantum computer to process multiple possible qubit states at once.4  
Exhibit 3: Superposition is the condition where a qubit can exist in a superposition of 0 and 1 
Illustrating the power of superposition

Source: BofA Global Research 
Note: A Bloch sphere is a geometric representation of qubit states. 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Error rates and fidelity 
Fidelity is a measure of how closely a quantum state or operation matches what was intended (Exhibit 4). A higher fidelity 
generally means fewer errors. 
Physical qubits are prone to errors. One way to address this is to combine multiple physical qubits to create a more reliable 
logical qubit. Quantum error correction (QEC) allows errors in the physical qubits to be detected and corrected, helping protect 
the information stored in the logical qubit.

4 Ibid.

### Page 4

4 21 October 2026 
  I NSTI TUTE

Exhibit 4: Fidelity measures how accurately a qubit retains its intended quantum state during computation 
Infographic showing the implications of high versus low fidelity

BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
How does quantum error correction work? 
At its simplest, QEC relies on redundancy, or storing information across multiple qubits so that errors can be detected and 
corrected.  
Consider an example: suppose someone wants to send a message represented by 0. Instead of sending a single 0, the message 
could be repeated three times as 000, known as a codeword. If one bit were accidentally flipped from 0 to 1 during transmission, 
the recipient might receive 010. Because two of the three bits are still 0, the recipient could infer that the intended message 
was 000. 
However, if two bits flipped, producing 110 or 011, the recipient could infer the wrong message. If all three flipped to 111, the 
recipient would have no way to know from the received codeword that errors had occurred.5 This approach is known as a 
repetition code and illustrates how redundancy can help protect information from errors.  
Repetition codes are a simple form of error correction that can address bit-flip errors, where a 0 changes to a 1 or vice versa. 
More sophisticated QEC codes are needed to address other quantum errors, such as phase flips.6 Shor’s code, developed by 
American theoretical computer scientist Peter Shor, uses nine physical qubits to encode one logical qubit and can correct one 
bit-flip error or one phase flip error, but it can’t correct both types of errors at the same time. Meanwhile, Steane’s seven-qubit 
code, developed by Oxford physicist Andrew Steane, can also correct both types of errors. It has the advantage of being fault-
tolerant, meaning the error-correction process itself does not introduce additional errors.7 
At what error level might we start seeing quantum advantage? 
Some classical computing systems today operate with extremely high reliability, often described as having "17 nines" of fidelity 
(99.99999999999999999%). By comparison, quantum computers currently achieve fidelity rates of around 99.9%. Gerald 
Mullally, CEO of OQC, has argued that quantum systems would need to add roughly three more nines of fidelity to achieve 
commercial relevance, or quantum advantage. 
Noise and decoherence 
Noise refers to unwanted disturbances that affect quantum systems and can lead to errors in computations (Exhibit 5). It can 
come from sources such as interactions with the environment, electromagnetic interference and temperature fluctuations. 
Coherence is a quantum system’s ability to maintain its quantum properties, including superposition and entanglement. When 
coherence is lost through interactions with the environment, the process is known as “decoherence,” causing the system to 
behave more classically (Exhibit 6).8

5 Microsoft Ignite. (n.d.) Introduction to quantum error correction.

To fully control qubits for calculations and information transfer, they need to be carefully protected from their surroundings. 
External factors like noise, energy, changes in Earth’s magnetic field, radiation from Wi-Fi or mobile phones or cosmic rays can 
interfere with the process. Even small disturbances can lead to decoherence, making it difficult to develop large-scale, fault-
tolerant quantum computers.9 
According to QuEra Computing, quantum noise can be mitigated through physical isolation of qubits, the development of more 
precise control techniques and quantum error correction, which helps detect and correct errors in physical qubits.  
Exhibit 5: Noise refers to unwanted disturbances, e.g., interactions 
with the environment, that lead to errors in quantum computation

properties, superposition and entanglement

BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Quantum processing unit (QPU) 
Referred to as the brain of the quantum computer, the QPU is the core hardware where qubits reside and where quantum 
computations take place. QPUs rely on quantum behaviors like superposition and entanglement to tackle problems in areas such 
as cryptography, simulation and optimization. Just as a CPU (central processing unit) in classical computing contains multiple 
components, a QPU includes quantum gates (operations that manipulate qubits in a controlled way, similar to logic gates in 
classical computing) and interconnects, which enable communication between qubits and other quantum processors.10 
Next, we’ll look at broader computing architecture… 
Heterogeneous computing  
Heterogeneous computing refers to a future computing landscape likely combining classical and quantum computing, potentially 
unlocking more powerful, faster and cheaper compute and helping solve problems that classical computers cannot. According to 
BofA Global Research, AI and quantum computing could also converge and complement each other, enabling more 
transformative use cases. 
Quantum modality  
A quantum modality refers to the type of physical technology used to create and manipulate qubits. We discuss the modalities in 
greater depth in the following section. 
Lastly, we outline qubit technologies… 
Neutral atom qubit 
A quantum modality that uses individual atoms with no electrical charge as qubits (Exhibit 7). The atoms are held in place using 
focused lasers known as optical tweezers. Neutral atom qubit systems do not require atoms to be ionized, which means fewer 
energy constraints and potentially simpler cooling requirements. Atoms such as rubidium or cesium are typically used. The qubits 
are manipulated by changing the energy levels or positions of the trapped atoms using laser pulses.

Photonic qubit 
A quantum modality that uses particles of light, known as photons, to carry quantum information (Exhibit 8).11 Optical 
components such as beam splitters, which divide light along different paths, and phase shifters, which change the phase of light, 
can be used to perform quantum operations. Photonic quantum computers can use integrated photonic chips to manipulate 
photons and enable quantum logic. 
Exhibit 8: A quantum modality relying on the quantum properties of light 
Key features of photonic qubits

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

11 Moore, J. (2025, March 28). The 6 different types of quantum computing technology. TechTarget.

### Page 7

21 October 2026    7

I NSTI TUTE

Spin qubit 
A quantum modality that uses the intrinsic spin of electrons or nuclei in solid-state materials, such as silicon or diamond. These 
spins can be manipulated using magnetic or electric fields, and their states are read using optical or charge-sensing techniques. 
Spin qubits are particularly appealing because they can leverage existing CMOS (complementary metal-oxide semiconductor; a 
technology to build processors) manufacturing infrastructure, making it easier to integrate them with conventional processors. 
Superconducting qubit 
A quantum modality in which electronic circuits create qubits. They are built using Josephson junctions, where two 
superconductors are separated by a thin insulating layer. Microwave pulses manipulate the qubits’ quantum states to run 
computations.12  
Specifically, a resistance-free current oscillates back and forth around a quantum loop. An injected microwave signal excites the 
current into superposition states.13 To enable this process, superconducting materials are cooled to near absolute zero (around 
10-20 millikelvin) using dilution refrigerators, helping reduce thermal noise and maintain coherence. 
Topological qubit 
A quantum modality that aims to encode quantum information using topological properties rather than relying only on individual 
particle states (Exhibit 9). One way to think about this is through the paths certain quasiparticles take as they move around one 
another, creating “braids” in space-time. Because the information can depend on the overall pattern of these braids rather than 
small details of their paths, the approach is designed to be more resilient to certain disturbances. 
Exhibit 9: Resilient to small perturbations but requires special quasiparticles not yet been conclusively demonstrated in nature 
An infographic of topological qubits

Source: BofA Global Research 
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Trapped-ion qubit  
A quantum modality in which ions (electrically charged atoms) are held in place using electromagnetic fields. These ions act as 
the qubits and are controlled by the system with tightly focused laser beams.14 The qubits are held inside ultra-high vacuum 
(UHV) chambers.

14 Moore, J. (2025, March 28). The 6 different types of quantum computing technology. TechTarget.

RBNZ の予想外の利上げ見送りで、NZ ドル相場は一段と弱含むか 
～追加利上げの時期や総選挙を巡る不透明感が重なり、豪ドルとの対照的な動きが続くか～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ ニュージーランド準備銀行（RBNZ）は、10 月 27～28 日に開催した定例理事会において、政
策金利を2.75％に据え置くことを決定した。RBNZ は７月に約３年ぶりの利上げに舵を切り、
９月にも追加利上げを実施するなど金融引き締めを強めてきた。このため、今回の据え置き
は３会合ぶりであり、金融市場の事前予想に反する決定となった。 
○ ７-９月のインフレ率は前年比＋3.7％と前期から鈍化したものの、コアインフレ率は加速す
るなど、インフレ圧力は依然として根強いことが確認された。このため、金融市場において
は RBNZ が今回利上げに動くことを織り込む動きが広がりをみせていた。こうしたなか、 今回
の決定は４対３の僅差となったほか、声明文や総裁会見でも追加利上げの必要性が示された
ものの、そのタイミングについては不透明感が残る。 
○ また、11 月７日の総選挙を控え、RBNZ の政策目標の見直しが争点となっていることも、 先行
きの政策運営を巡る不透明感につながっている。金融市場では、予想外の金利据え置きに対
する 「失望」 に加え、 金融政策が後手に回るとの警戒感もＮＺドル相場の重しとなっている。
当面はＮＺドル相場の上値が重い展開が続くと見込まれ、オセアニア通貨のなかでも豪ドル
とＮＺドルが対照的な動きをみせる可能性がある。

【RBNZは事前予想に反して、10 月の定例会合で３会合ぶりに政策金利を据え置く決定】 
ニュージーランド準備銀行（RBNZ）は、10 月 27～28 日に開催した定例理事会において、政策金
利であるオフィシャル・キャッシュ・レート（ＯＣＲ）を2.75％に据え置くことを決定した。ニュ
ージーランドでは、2025 年以降にインフレ率が加速し、RBNZ の定める目標レンジ（１～３％）の
上限を上回る伸びで推移している。 さらに、 イラン情勢の悪化を受けた原油価格の上昇がエネルギ
ーインフレを招いたことから、RBNZ は７月の定例会合で約３年ぶりの利上げに舵を切り、 ９月会合
でも追加利上げに動くなど、金融引き締めを強化してきた（注１）。このため、RBNZ による金利据

伸びとなるなど、 インフレ圧力が高まっている様子が確認された。このため、 金融市場ではRBNZ が

【僅差での据え置き決定、市場の「失望」を反映してNZドル相場は上値の重い展開が続くか】 
会合後に公表された声明文では、今回の決定について「４（据え置き）対３（25bp の利上げ）の
賛成多数で金利据え置きを決定した」とした。足元の物価動向について「想定を下回った」 、 「賃金
上昇が抑制されており、 中期的なインフレ期待は安定している」 とする一方、 「コアインフレ率は目
標レンジの上限近くに加速しており、物価上昇の動きが広範に 及びつつある」との見方を示した。
先行きについては「世界的な情勢が短期的にインフレ圧力を一段と強めている」として、その要因
に「原油高によるエネルギー価格の上昇と通貨ＮＺドル安」 を挙げた。 そして、 「10-12 月のインフ
レ率は＋4.4％に加速が見込まれる」としつつ、 「原油や石油製品価格の変動が激しく、インフレ見
通しは不透明」との見方を示した。 
実体経済については「回復が続いているものの、状況には依然ばらつきがある」との見方を示し
た。具体的には「外需関連産業では、商品市況の上昇、世界的な需要の堅調さ、外国人観光客数の
増加の恩恵を受けている」一方、 「内需関連産業は低調に推移している」とした。一方で、 「オーク
ランドを中心に住宅投資の回復の兆しがでている」とした。 
政策運営については「物価に対する一時的な要因を差し引いた上で、 経済に不必要な不安定を起
こすことを回避しつつ、中期的にインフレ率を目標レンジの中央（２％）に戻すことに注力してい
る」とあらためて強調した。 そのうえで、 「家計消費が弱く、 一部の企業ではコスト上昇の転嫁が制
限されている」 としつつ、 「インフレ率が高止まりすれば、 コスト上昇がインフレ期待や価格や賃金
に波及してインフレがより持続的なものとなるリスクが高まる」との見方を示した。 
世界経済については「堅調に推移しているものの、地政学リスクやエネルギーインフレ、金融市
場のボラティリティの高まり、ＡＩ（人工知能）関連投資の急増などを背景に、その脆弱性は高ま
っている」との見方を示した。一方で、同国経済について「回復の動きが予想以上に力強くなる可
能性はある一方、世界経済の悪化、外需の好調さが雇用や所得、家計消費に波及しなければ勢いを
失う可能性がある」との認識を示した。そのうえで、今回の決定について「政策委員は全体的なイ
ンフレ見通しでは一致したが、個々のリスクに対する見方や金融政策の対応時期に関する意見は割
れた」、先行きについても「追加利上げが必要となる可能性は高いとの見方で一致したものの、 その
タイミングは経済見通し次第」として、追加利上げに含みを持たせる考えを示した。 
会合後に記者会見に臨んだブレマン総裁は、今回の協議について「景気動向を勘案すれば、より
抑制的な政策が必要になることで合意した」 、 「ＮＺドル安が続けば輸入インフレを助長する可能性
がある」との認識が共有されたとした。先行きについては「さらなる引き締めが必要となり、状況
によっては引き締め的な領域に入る可能性がある」 、 「今後の会合で追加利上げを行う可能性が高い」
との認識を示した。そして、 「ＯＣＲの道筋が９月会合の想定を上回る可能性についても協議した」
としつつ、 「中立金利の見通しには幅があり、 常に不確実性がある」 として、 具体的な水準への言及
は避けた。一方で、 今回の決定については「経済見通しに基づいた決定である」 としたうえで、 「イ

### Page 4

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
4/4 
ンフレ率を目標レンジに戻すための条件は依然として良好」 、 「賃金上昇率は目標とするインフレ動
向に見合う水準を下回っている」との認識を示した。 
前述したように、金融市場においては今回の利上げを織り込む動きが広がっていたため、予想外
の金利据え置き決定に対しては『失望』
にも似た反応がみられ、 ＮＺドル相場は
調整の動きを強めている （図３） 。背景に
は、 総選挙の行方が不透明であることに
加え、その後の 12 月に開催される次回
の定例会合において、 追加利上げが実施
されるか否かについても不透明感が残
ることがある。一部では、金融政策が後
手に回る（behind the curve）との警戒
感が強まっていることも影響している
と考えられる。このため、当面のＮＺド
ル相場は弱含む展開が続くと予想され、 オセアニア通貨では豪ドルが強含む一方、ＮＺドルは上値
の重い展開が続くなど、両通貨が対照的な動きをみせる可能性が高まっている。

《商品貨幣受制於強勢美元，人民幣溫和升值》
美元在9月FOMC會議再升一碼之後，美聯儲獨立
性有所增強，加上市場對於美國升息預計至明年
中仍有三碼之多，美元再次走強，DXY站上100。
市場對於做多澳幣有所遲疑，市場頭寸再次轉向
空頭主導，但我們認爲包括金和銅在內的大宗商
品價格的反彈令貿易條件的改善，澳幣在0.71以
下已經有了買入價值。人民幣進入年尾出口商有
結匯需求，惟留意中美利差擴大等因素制約，預
計4Q26人民幣偏向升值但空間溫和。

《投資關鍵-澳洲貨幣政策取決地緣與油價變化》
澳聯儲主席布洛克在 2026年9月22日的最新講話中明
確表示，4.5%的失業率目前“仍然偏緊”，失業率需
要升至4.5%~5.0%之間，才能有效為勞動力市場降溫
並緩解通膨壓力；然隨後公布的 8月失業率由 4.5%升
至4.7%，已落在央行目標區間。不過澳洲8月份CPI數
據，年增由3.5%升至4.0%為澳央9月再度升息的主因 ，
主要受到能源價格漲所致；然整體結構未見失控風險 ，
核心CPI年增率持平於3.6%，扣除扣居住與交通CPI年
增由2.2%降至2.1%，因此12月份會期升息與否取決
於油價能否回落 ， 如 地 緣 趨 緩 配 合 失 業 率 維 持 在
4.5~5.0%區間，澳央12月有暫停升息空間。

另外，觀察CFTC投機頭寸顯示，澳幣投機空頭已經大
量累積，做空倉位正在主導市場 ，短期認爲澳幣應在
0.7~0.71的目標價盤整，更多上行空間應來自於美元
降息預期的重新開啓 ，或更多在明年中期左右 。我們
認爲澳幣的看法仍然中性略為正向，而近期跌破0.7關
卡的價位下，有些許布局空間。
• 我們認爲澳幣短線中性偏正向，
在澳幣短期有所回撤 0.7以下價
位下，可適時加碼澳幣部位。

紐西蘭景氣修復逐步延續，中期復甦動能仍在
《投資關鍵-美元強勢壓抑紐元表現 ，惟中期復甦動能
仍在》
9月份紐元表現主要受外部因素主導 。儘管紐西蘭經濟
數據顯示改善跡象，但油價上升及美國財政赤字憂慮推
動美國長天期公債殖利率升至逾二十年高位，帶動美元
走強及市場重新評估美國利率前景 ，令NZD/USD由月
初接近0.59水平回落至0.56附近，紐元與其他高貝他貨
幣同步受壓。
經濟基本面方面 ，紐西蘭第二季 GDP季增0.2%、年增
2.6%，優於央行預期 ，顯示經濟持續復甦 。企業投資

及AI相關無形資產支出保持穩健，惟家庭消費及零售活
動仍然偏弱，反映內需復甦步伐相對溫和。貨幣政策方
面，市場預期RBNZ將於10月維持官方現金利率不變，
以評估既有緊縮政策的影響；惟若經濟活動及通膨壓力
持續展現韌性，年底前不排除進一步收緊政策的可能。
在主要已開發國家央行政策路徑逐漸分化的背景下，紐
西蘭相對較高的利率水準仍可為紐元提供一定支持，但
短期市場焦點預料仍集中於美國利率及美元走勢。
展望未來一至兩季，我們認為紐元仍有望受惠於國內經
濟成長改善及投資活動回暖，逐步擺脫近期低位。然而，
在美國利率維持較高水準及美元強勢延續的環境下，紐
元上行空間可能受到一定抑制 。因此，我們雖仍預期
NZD/USD 可自目前水準溫和回升，但將第四季目標價
由0.60下調至0.59，以反映美元強勢環境可能維持較長
時間。
• 展望未來一至兩季 ，我們認為
紐元有望受惠於國內經濟成長
改善及投資活動回暖 ，逐步擺
脫近期低位 。然而 ，美國公債
殖利率高企及美元韌性較預期
強勁，料限制其升值空間。

通膨數據回落，6月降息的可能性加大重點摘要 《投資關鍵-政策觀望與美元強勢夾擊 ，加幣延續弱
勢格局》
中東局勢未見緩和，油價及通膨壓力維持高檔，促使
聯準會於9月16日升息25個基點，市場焦點亦由降息
預期轉向利差優勢及避險需求，導致非美貨幣承壓。
在市場風險情緒急轉直下，加幣自 USD/CAD 1.385
附近持續走弱，並一度來到 1.42以上。
加拿大央行料維持審慎觀望立場，市場對其轉向鷹派
的預期仍缺乏數據支持。儘管油價上升可能推升整體
通膨，但剔除能源因素後，加拿大經濟基本面尚未出
現足以支持進一步收緊政策的明顯改善。加上近期市
場已大幅回補加元空頭部位，在下一份就業報告公布
前，預料難有促使加拿大央行改變政策基調的催化劑，
因此貨幣政策前景仍偏向中性。
另一方面，高油價有助提升加拿大能源出口收入，對
加幣形成一定支撐；惟中東地緣壓力和美加貿易不確
定性猶存，料將拖慢加拿大經濟及就業復甦步伐。綜
合上述因素，我們預期加幣短期走勢仍偏疲弱，故將
4Q26美元兌加幣目標價由1.37上調至1.39。

《投資關鍵 –人民幣迎結匯需求與中美利差擴大的拉鋸》
人民幣與美元指數過去維持相關性，惟2025年下半年開
啟人民幣中期獨立升值行情。儘管兩者走勢高度相似，
但斜率拉開差距，人民幣升值動能顯著強於貶值方向。
今年以來中國景氣K型化樣態持續，出口景氣能見度高
於內需，累積大量貿易順差所引導結匯需求支持人民幣。
惟留意中國內需疲軟依舊，川習會落幕後，中國官方政
策焦點轉向穩增長與五中全會。除房市救市政策積極推
出外，中國人行宣布下調PSL 利率一碼至1.5%。中期來
看人民幣升值動能或受兩因素制約，首先中國經濟週期
挑戰加深不排除有資金流出壓力。其次，由於市場對美
國聯準會的升息預期加強，與中國寬鬆貨幣政策有所差
異，美中利差恐不利人民幣表態。
總結來看，4Q26人民幣將在結匯需求 、中美利差擴大

等因素拉鋸 ， 預 計 人 民 幣 走 勢 偏 向 溫 和 升 值 ，
USD/CNY Q4 6.65， 明年Q1 6.55。

### Page 6

《投資關鍵 -南非幣短線承壓，利差優勢支撐中期前
景》
南非幣近期承壓 ，美元兌南非幣由月初約 16.1升至
16.7水準，主要受美債殖利率攀升及美元轉強拖累 。
市場重新定價美國高利率將維持更久 ，帶動新興市
場資產普遍承壓 。此外，南非債市外資流出及避險
需求升溫 ，進一步加劇南非幣波動 ，使其表現落後
多數主要新興市場貨幣。
儘管外部環境轉趨逆風 ，南非國內經濟基本面仍展
現一定韌性 。近期零售銷售及消費數據表現穩健 ，
支持經濟溫和復甦 。雖然經常帳赤字 、外部需求疲
弱及油價上升、聖嬰現象（El Niño）帶來的糧食供
給風險 ，可能增加未來通膨壓力 ，但亦提高南非央
行維持偏鷹立場的必要性 。我們認為 ，在內需仍具
韌性及通膨風險升溫下 ，南非央行短期不急於轉向
寬鬆，較高利率環境可望持續提供南非幣利差支撐 。
短期而言 ，南非幣仍將受到美國利率 、美元走勢及
全球風險情緒主導 。若美債殖利率維持高檔 ，南非
幣可能持續面臨波動與資金流出壓力 。然而，相較
其他新興市場貨幣 ，我們對南非幣中期前景略趨正

面。一方面，南非央行偏鷹立場有助維持利差優勢；
另一方面 ，黃金價格回穩 、新興市場外匯波動率處
於相對低位 ，以及穩定的套息交易需求 ，均有利南
非幣表現。
重點摘要
資料來源：StatsSA，JPM， 2026/9，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/9/30

資結果自行負責。未經本公司許可，本資料及訊息不得逕行抄錄、翻印或另作派發。本行以上市場資訊與分析，不涉及特定投資標的之建議。
南非貨幣政策立場料繼續趨審慎偏鷹
• 南非通膨降溫主要來自燃料及
食品價格等波動性較高的項目 ，
中期通膨壓力尚未完全消退。
• 若南非央行政策立場不及市場
預期鷹派 ，利率路徑面臨重新
定價的風險 ，恐對南非幣構成
壓力 。整體而言 ，我們對南非

幣維持中性偏審慎看法。
